\section{cs.AI}
\arxivwithtarget{2608.00001}{Revisiting Classic Thought Experiments to Measure Consciousness for Artificial Intelligence Safety}{Peter David Fagan}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00003}{AutoFOAM: The Self-Refining Autonomous OpenFOAM Agent}{Arun Govind Neelan, A Seshaditya}\textbf{cs.AI}.

AutoFOAM 提出一个基于 large language model 的 agent 框架, 旨在解决 OpenFOAM 这类 CFD 仿真工具配置繁琐、依赖专业经验的问题. 该 agent 以 Qwen-coder 2.5-14B 为底座, 在 7 个 OpenFOAM 求解器与 13 个参数化 mesh 模板上微调, 并引入 y plus-aware numerical policy, 从而仅凭自然语言指令即可自动完成模型构建、运行与评估. 其核心


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00006}{Enhancing LLMs with Context-Specific Knowledge for Mitigating Misinformation in SMEs: A RAG-based Modeling and Analysis}{Md. Samiul Islam, Iqbal H. Sarker, Chadni Islam, Ahmad Mohsin, Ahmed Ibrahim, Helge Janicke}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00008}{Energy Efficiency of Locally Deployed LLMs: A Preliminary Quantitative GPU Power Benchmark on Consumer Hardware}{Philipp M. Zähl, Anika Hennig}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.ET, cs.PF.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00014}{CoT-Core: Accelerating LLM Evaluation via CoT-Aware Coreset Selection}{Qihua Pan, Zhenheng Tang, Peijie Dong, Xiang Liu, Huacan Wang, Bo Li, Xiaowen Chu}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00015}{Optimization and Constraint Modeling using LLMs with a Retrieval Augmented Generation Process}{Prateek Roy, Akash Singirikonda}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本文针对LLM在optimization modeling与constraint modeling中常生成结构不一致或不完整formulation的问题, 提出了一种基于Retrieval Augmented Generation的pipeline. 作者利用Text2Zinc种子描述和LLM生成的professional personas合成500个带验证Python solver脚本的optimization problems, 存入Chroma vector database, 并在推理时检索语义相似的样例作为LangChain agent的上下文. 在Qwen 3 30B Instruct模型上的三个测试集上, 准确率从40%提升至72%(NL4OPT) , 以及从40%到56%、32%到56%(MAMO Easy与Complex) , 表明利用语义验证的synthetic examples结合retrieval augmentation可有效替代fine-tuning, 提升解的准确性与结构可靠性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00017}{Memory Reward Inflation in Self-Improving LLM Agents}{Mohammad Asadolahi, Amir Amini, Samira Talebi, Amirfarhad Farhadi, Azadeh Zamanifar}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CE.

本文针对self-improving LLM agents在无权重更新条件下利用外部记忆存储episode并依据LLM评分作为proxy reward时出现的reward inflation问题, 指出该偏差源于ground-truth不可得时错误episode被高估, 且记忆复用使误差累积而非抵消, 由此形式化Error-Independence Assumption (EIA), 并证明其是校正inflation的必要条件, 即可用信号需同时追踪truth且其error与memory bias解相关, 恢复收益是这两个量的closed-form函数. 进一步证明inflation在按stored score检索及plain similarity retrieval下均会加剧. 提出的无答案de-inflation算法LUCID在BIRD text-to-SQL benchmark上一致提升执行准确率至$56.9\%$, 高于Memento式self-graded agent的$54.0\%$和无记忆baseline的$52.4\%$.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00026}{Request-Level Energy Attribution for Batched LLM Serving}{Qi Luo, Kunlin Li, Ziwen Wang, Dongsheng Wang, Yun Chen}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.DC.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00027}{Motif-Mamba: network motif improved mamba for long-range sequence modeling}{Chonghe Hao, Yue Sun, Jian Zhang, Yansong Wang, Wangzi Yao, Yunjie Yao, Tielin Zhang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00029}{Nova: An End-to-End MLIR Compiler for Deep Learning}{Adwaid Suresh, Aparna A, Harshini V M, Jona Delcy C A, Killi Uma Maheswara Rao, Ram Charan Golla, Surendra Vendra}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.AR, cs.LG, cs.PL.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00033}{SIRIN: A Unified Toolkit for Detecting Contextual Hallucinations in Retrieval-Augmented and Memory-Grounded LLM Systems}{Julia Belikova, Rauf Parchiev, Mikhail Filimonov, Konstantin Polev, Andrey Savchenko, Maksim Makarenko}\textbf{cs.AI}.

SIRIN是一个统一工具包与交互式Web UI, 用于检测检索增强、agentic与memory-grounded LLM系统中的contextual hallucination, 即那些流畅但缺乏证据支撑的响应. 该方法将representation probing、uncertainty estimation与judge-style verification三类检测范式统一在单一接口与评估流程下, 并支持白盒与黑盒设置中的response级和span级检查. SIRIN还整合了pre-generation query answerability任务, 并通过轻量插件设计便于扩展新检测器. 实验展示了其在hallucination detection、query answerability以及作为长期记忆系统faithfulness gate方面的有效性, 代码已公开.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00035}{Linguistic Context Recodes Visual Representations in Vision-Language Models}{Brian Song, Michael A. Lepori, Ellie Pavlick}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CV.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00054}{RAG-TESTER: Automated End-to-End Testing of Retrieval-Augmented Large Language Models}{Ange Maiztegi, Jon Ayerdi, Miren Illarramendi, Aitor Arrieta}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.SE.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00065}{H+ Embedding: Harmonizing Global and Token-Level Retrieval with Context-Dependent Phrases}{Shusen Zhang, Junyi Hu, Ye Feng, Ziteng Wang, Zhaoyuan Pan, Guosheng Dong, Xiaojun Yuan, Jiangshou Hong, Xiangzhi Wang}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00101}{Agentic Coding in the Wild: Characterizing GitHub Copilot Traces at Production Scale}{Banruo Liu, Haoran Qiu, Íñigo Goiri, Rodrigo Fonseca, Ricardo Bianchini, Esha Choukse}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.

该论文针对GitHub Copilot这类agentic coding workload进行首次生产级刻画, 因为其与chatbot workload在结构上根本不同, 现有LLM serving系统假设可能失效. 基于3.2M用户与761M次LLM调用的trace, 研究发现每个用户turn内自治agent loop的KV cache命中率平均达90%, 但跨turn降至55%, 并在模型切换或context compaction后急剧失效. 进一步观察到token消耗、turn时长与tool调用呈长尾分布, 且turn间用户空闲可达数分钟, 据此设计了轻量级idle-time predictor, 能捕获86-90%的总空闲时间. 这些结论为agent-native基础设施的资源编排提供了经验基础并挑战了现有服务系统设计.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00102}{Can LLM Agents Price Competitively? A Dynamic Multi-Attribute Auction Benchmark for Agentic Commerce}{Shimaa Ahmed, Yiwei Cai, Mohsen Minaei, Rahul Rachuri}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.MA.

Agentic commerce 的落地需要 LLM agent 具备真实市场中的动态定价能力, 但现有研究未系统检验其在隐藏偏好、对手实时适应和需求冲击下的表现. 本文提出 Bazaar, 一种基于 closed-form 客户效用的 dynamic sealed-bid multi-attribute auction benchmark, 可精确评估 agent 的定价策略. 实验发现 11 个 frontier LLM 在客户获取和利润上排名不一致, 需求冲击后先前学习最快的 agent 反而调整最慢, 且最强 agent 的利润不足 hindsight-optimal 的三分之一. 结果表明当前 LLM agent 在商务场景中虽有进展, 但利润优化仍有显著提升空间.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00122}{Shared Organizational Memory for Enterprise Coding Agents: System Design and Deployment Snapshot}{Harsh Rao Dhanyamraju, Leonidas Raghav}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00155}{AgentStream: How Well Do Self-Evolving LLM Agents Perform Under Streaming Tasks?}{Dong Yan, Jian Liang, Dapeng Hu, Ran He, Nicholas Jing Yuan, Qi Zhang, Tieniu Tan}\textbf{cs.AI}.

现有自进化 LLM Agent 评估多采用独立单任务设置, 忽视了真实场景中任务流动态变化的特性. 为此, 作者提出 AgentStream 框架, 将多个 agentic benchmark 配置为可调任务流, 并实例化 Isolated、Sequential 与 Interleaved 三种 streaming 场景以控制系统复杂度与领域组成. 通过组合评估五种代表性 self-evolving 方法在三种基础模型上的表现, 发现自进化可靠性随场景改变, 且收益受模型能力门槛限制并呈非单调关系, 不存在跨模型与场景均占优的方法. 该结论为在实际任务流下选择 self-evolving 方法提供了依据, 主张应放弃孤立评估而采用更真实的任务流评价范式.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00200}{TRACE-TS: Attribution-Grounded and Traceable Sensor-Language Reasoning for Human Activity Understanding}{Sparsh Rastogi, Tanmay Kumar, Baiyu Chen, Jatin Bedi, Zechen Li, Flora D. Salim}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL, cs.ET, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00215}{Personalizing Large Language Model Agents with Small Policy Models}{Dian Jin, Zhi Zhang, Huichao Li, Yihe Pan, Rundong Huang, Doudou Zhou}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对冻结的大语言模型(LLM) agent, 个性化其执行决策(如记忆检索、工具调用、提问和响应风格) 传统上依赖微调或prompt工程, 但前者成本高昂或不可行, 后者无法从反馈中直接调整执行策略. 本文提出FABLE(Factorized Adaptive Bandit Layer for Execution) , 一个置于黑盒host agent外部的轻量policy layer, 将个性化建模


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00243}{More Debate, Same Evidence: Structural Limits of Homogeneous Multi-Agent Groundedness}{Yuelyu Ji}\textbf{cs.AI}.

该论文检验了同构多智能体辩论假设, 即LLM judge间的相互批判能否提升groundedness verification的可靠性. 在六个公开基准上, 三人panel相对固定单模型参考的系统级准确率差异为$+8.5$到$-4.4$个百分点, 其中两个数据集有稳定增益、一个有稳定损失、三个统计不显著. 由于对比系统使用不同模型变体, 结果反映完整系统差异而非纯粹辩论效应, 表明同质多agent panel未必能带来一致可靠改进.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00303}{CrystalMem: Elastic Memory for Self-Evolving LLM Agents via Knowledge Crystallization}{Beining Wu, Jun Huang}\textbf{cs.AI}.

该论文针对自进化LLM agent在弹性内存预算下能力无法随预算恢复的memory hysteresis现象, 证明了仅采用保留或丢弃条目的策略必然存在residual-deficit floor, 因此提出CrystalMem, 一个将entries按crystallization-energy schedule在四种fidelity states间降级, 并以advantage-weighted influence和dependency coupling排序demotions的弹性内存sidecar, 同时在显式compute与byte caps下通过verified recrystallization恢复能力. 在七个环境、十七种方法和六个backbones上, CrystalMem在每种设置中都取得最高restored capability, 并在50% byte budget下匹配最强baseline在full provision时的表现, 同预算下平均领先+4.6 pp.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00321}{Trust and Its Betrayal under Three Representational Strategies}{Mihnea C. Moldoveanu, Joel A.C. Baum}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00326}{Learning to Coordinate Symbolic Tools: LLM Agents for Verified Sum-of-Squares Certificates}{Bohan Chen, Shivam N. Patel, Richard Hoffmann, Sam Looi, Tony Yue Yu}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本文研究如何让LLM agent通过调用外部符号工具(如SymPy) 来生成可验证的weighted sum-of-squares (SOS) decomposition, 从而证明多项式非负性. 由于候选分解可被精确检查但搜索空间庞大且需要协调多种代数变换, 作者构建了135万条覆盖八个子任务的合成语料, 先进行supervised fine-tuning (SFT) 模拟工具调用轨迹, 再使用GRPO结合符号奖励优化策略. 最终系统在相同生成器下的合成问题上达到78.96%的verified success(base model仅44.73%) , 并在九个多项式任务上取得91.75%的macro accuracy, 展示了结合领域技能训练、可执行工具与verifier反馈的可靠agent设计范式.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00335}{RMSWeb: Reflection, Failure-Mode Mining, and Salvage-DS for Web Agent Reinforcement Learning}{Chengbo Liu, Lifang Zhou, Ruijie Yan, Pei Tan, Ao Sun, Haojun Huang, Guichun Hua, Sining Wei, Yining Chen, Yingying He, Yutao Xie}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL, cs.LG.

针对web agent在SFT后训练强化学习(RL)时面临的数据收集成本高、成功轨迹含低效绕路、以及group-relative RL中action-level reward信号弱或误导的问题, 本文提出RMSWeb配方, 用于Qwen3-VL-Instruct的8B与32B模型. 其核心方法包括: 基于reflection的有条件重试提升数据收集产出并缩短轨迹; 通过failure-mode mining将offline RL聚焦于SFT策略暴露的关键状态; 以及Salvage-DS, 结合action-semantic polarized reward、contrast-and-competence-gated动态采样和对被拒绝组仅使用action anchor的fallback信号. 实验表明, 在WebVoyager、Online-Mind2Web和WebTailBench上, RMSWeb相比SFT提升2.4-7.0点(8B)和1.2-7.7点(32B), 同时以更少的action steps完成任务, 并在同规模开源模型中取得

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00711}{Tracing the Cascade: A Topology-Aware Evaluation Framework for Scientific Agent Hallucinations}{Xinshun Feng, Ziqi Miao, Lijun Li, Jing Shao}\textbf{cs.AI}.

SCHEMA 针对科学领域 LLM agents 的幻觉评估缺陷, 现有基准孤立处理事实且采用统一准确率, 忽视了科学知识的高连通拓扑结构, 导致单一错误可沿多步推理传播并污染完整轨迹. 该方法自动从 benchmark seeds 与文献证据构建 scientific concept graph, 生成图基任务, 并采用 trajectory hallucination pipeline 以拓扑加权严重度评分审计中间推理, 同时用 multi-agent counterfactual attribution 定位失败因果机制. 实验发现幻觉集中在少数高连接知识枢纽, 且最终答案准确性与轨迹真实性脱钩, 模型常通过结构错误的推理得出正确结论, 表明对


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00717}{AI-Based Thesis Assessment: An Empirical Study of Human Evaluation Priorities and Their Impact on Automated Assessment}{Garv Vikram Gursahaney, Baskhad Idrisov, Thorsten Fröhlich, Tim Schlippe}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00745}{Behavioral Grammar: Detecting Adaptive Malware via Tiny Language Model Priors and Second-Order Temporal Analysis}{Zihan Luo}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00764}{FinDeepIndicator: Benchmarking Deep Research Agents in End-to-End Financial Indicator Construction}{Chaoqun Yang, Fengbin Zhu, Xinyu Lin, Long Bai, Xiaoluan Liu, Ke-Wei Huang, Roger Zimmermann, Tat-Seng Chua}\textbf{cs.AI}.

现有金融benchmark多关注answer-level准确率且预设数据已提供, 忽略了指标构建的中间过程. 为此提出FinDeepIndicator, 首个专用于评估Deep Research agent在端到端financial indicator construction中表现的benchmark, 覆盖formula specification、data collection、indicator calculation和answer generation四个阶段, 包含3,350个QA对及中美市场10年数据. 实验显示search-equipped LLM在公式推理上尚可, 但在数据检索与数值执行环节准确率显著下降; DR agent整体更优, 但可靠性仍不足, 这为提升finance领域DR agent的trustworthiness提供了关键洞察.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00794}{Measurement Without Validity: The Compounding Reliability Problem in Agentic AI Evaluation}{William Caban}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本文针对Agentic AI评估中“有效性缺失”问题, 指出当前基于自动化benchmark的评分系统性不可靠, 其根源在于任务生成、LLM模拟用户与评判三个环节的效度损失以乘性方式复合叠加, 而非简单相加. 作者通过审计发现10个流行benchmark中7个存在效度缺陷, LLM模拟器间方差最高达9个百分点, 且55篇论文中约82%使用了结构不匹配或缺失的inter-rater reliability指标. 研究形式化给出$V_{\text{total}} \leq V_1 \times V_2 \times V_3$的上界约束, 并证明当同一模型家族贯穿三层时相关性失效会进一步收紧该界. 最终提出基于心理测量学的八项处方, 包括simulation calibration floor $\text{ICC}(A,1) \geq 0.70$、按后果等级分层的信度阈值($\alpha \geq 0.67/0.70/0.80$) 以及将IRR设为强制报告字段, 强调现有测量工具足以支撑改进.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00805}{AgentSLABench: Evaluating and Benchmarking Agentic Systems Under Resource Constraints}{Meher Bhaskar Madiraju, Meher Sai Preetam Madiraju}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00817}{Large language models improve physician accuracy but lead to false reliance}{Tirtha Chanda, Christoph Wies, Franziska Schramm, Carina Nogueira Garcia, Nicolas B. Merl, Martin J. Hetz, Jochen S. Utikal, Phillip Tschandl, Cristian Navarrete-Dechent, Alexander Thiem, Jakob N. Kather, Consortium, Titus J. Brinker}\textbf{cs.AI}, stat.AP.

该研究针对检索增强大语言模型(LLM)在临床决策中提供的source-linked assistance是否真正引导医生而非误导依赖的问题, 构建了agentic retrieval-augmented LLM系统CORA. 通过将支持性引用与模型输出绑定, CORA在保持基准性能的同时, 对训练数据截止后的新案例提升更大. 46名医生的实验显示, 准确率从unaided的70.8%升至82.6%, 且正确的引用能显著提高正确建议的采纳率(34%到76.9%) , 但错误的LLM回答一旦获得引用支持, 医生对其的抵制率从92%骤降至34.8%, 揭示了grounding-dependent safety risk这一关键缺陷.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00882}{Assuming You Knew: Fixing an Epistemic Semantics for Flow Policies Using Agentic AI}{David A. Naumann}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CR, cs.LO, cs.PL.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00891}{CADIR: A Cross-Backend Editable Intermediate Representation for Agentic CAD Generation}{Yu Liu, Jingzhe Ni, Yiming Chen, Junqi Huang, Ruofeng Tong, Min Tang, Peng Du}\textbf{cs.AI}.

CADIR 针对现有 CAD 生成方法难以同时保留 construction history、稳定拓扑引用与 feature-level editability 的问题, 提出一种基于 OCCT 几何内核的可执行中间表示. 该表示通过 construction graph 显式记录建模操作、参数依赖、约束与拓扑选择, 并引入 Geometric Signature Matching 来在参数变化和不同 CAD 后端之间稳定匹配边、面, 从而支持在 FreeCAD、SolidWorks 和 Fusion 360 中重建可编辑的原生特征历史. 此外, CADIR 还支持基于文本或图像的 full-graph 与 subgraph 检索, 使 agent 能复用完整模型或局部建模子结构. 实验表明, 相较


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00929}{Modeling Social Dynamics with an LLM-Enabled Agent Based Network-Dynamic (LAND) Model}{Lynnette Hui Xian Ng, Kathleen M. Carley}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本文提出一种名为LAND的混合式LLM-enabled Agent-Based Network-Dynamic模型, 用于仿真大规模社交网络中群体行为的涌现过程, 以解决传统agent模型难以刻画网络拓扑与话语交互耦合的问题. 作者基于GhostField架构构建AuraSight场景, 让314,244个异构agents在30天内交换529,327条消息, 并在ego-network topology、semantic network evolution、coordination dynamics与influence dynamics四个层面分析涌现行为. 结果表明, 仿真能够复现真实社交动态, 且协调与影响力并非源于个体agent, 而是由网络结构与叙事交换的递归作用共同产生, 验证了该框架作为社会动力学研究工具的可靠性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00962}{PMMC: Prospective Multimodal Memory Compilation for Long-Term LVLM Agents}{Jingyu Sun, Yan Lin, Yuyang Xue, Yifan Wang, Zhengtao Yao, Rui Qian, Zefeng Xu, Jiachen Li, Xianyang Liu, Jiancheng Pan, Jingyuan Sun, Syed Murtuza Baker, Hongpeng Zhou}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00967}{TrajWiki: Source-Grounded Memory Trajectories for Long-Horizon Dialogue Agents}{Jingyu Sun, Yuyang Xue, Mingyang Li, Zhengtao Yao, Jiachen Li, Yang Cui, Wenhao Cai, Haozhe Liu, Fangying Wang, Magdalene Katharina Montgomery, Syed Murtuza Baker, Hongpeng Zhou}\textbf{cs.AI}.

该论文针对长时对话Agent缺乏可追溯、可更新且诊断透明的外部记忆的问题, 提出TrajWiki框架. 其核心方法是将每条记忆建模为基于source-grounded的演化轨迹, 通过不可变的episodic snapshots及ADD、REVISE、DEPRECATE操作维护, 并引入Memory Wiki作为持久中间层, 将对话历史增量编译为结构化的interlinked wiki页面以降低检索成本. 推理时, 查询从相关wiki页面层级路由至记忆轨迹及对应源消息, 实现证据支撑的答案生成. 在LoCoMo和MedMT上的实验表明, TrajWiki在开源与闭源LLM backbone上均提升了长时对话性能, 并增强了记忆演化、检索失败与生成过程的interpretability和diagnostic visibility.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00969}{PROGRESS: Coverage-guided RL to Train Search-augmented LLM Agent}{Sudipta Paul, Vijay Srinivasan, Vivek Kulkarni, Aounon Kumar, Yashas Malur Saidutta, Wenbo Li, Srinivas Chappidi}\textbf{cs.AI}.

现有search-augmented LLM agent的训练主要依赖outcome-level rewards, 缺乏对search behavior的细粒度监督, 导致agent难以合理分解复杂查询. PROGRESS引入teacher-guided coverage reward, 利用冻结的teacher model将复杂查询分解为essential search queries, 并在R1-style training中显式指导policy model的查询分解决策, 而无需dense process-level supervision. 实验表明, 这种coverage-guided RL能显著提升整体task performance, 证明了显式监督agentic LLM的搜索策略对reliability至关重要.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00974}{Search-GRT: Guided Retrieval Training of Search Agents to Optimize for Complex Question Answering}{Aounon Kumar, Sudipta Paul, Vivek Kulkarni, Vijay Srinivasan, Srinivas Chappidi}\textbf{cs.AI}.

该论文针对大型语言模型(LLM) 在复杂 multi-hop question answering 中因早期检索不佳导致级联错误的问题, 提出 Guided Retrieval Training(GRT) 方法. 作者利用 ground truth 信息在 RL 训练时限制检索范围, 从而为 search agent 提供更密集的学习信号, 缓解稀疏奖励带来的训练困难, 并提升子查询生成与答案合成的准确性. 实验表明, GRT 在多种 QA 任务上一致优于 Search-R1, 尤其在 multi-hop QA 上性能提升超过 40%, 且能以更少的训练步骤达到更高准确率.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00979}{Passing Coarse Marginal Checks Can Be Cheap: Persona Mixtures and Imprecise Treatment-Response Estimates in an LLM Persona Panel}{Yohei Nakajima}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL, cs.GT.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00981}{Auditing Discovery Claims: A Two-Sided Criterion for Agentic Science, with the Negative Side Decidable}{Wenhui Chen, Jianlin Chen, Ziyao Lin, Chi Man Vong}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00991}{SCHEDBench: A Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Constraint Faithfulness in Natural-Language Combinatorial Scheduling}{Shrenil Shaun Sharma, Avi Sharma}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01000}{Judging Is Not Enumerating: Silent Omissions in LLM-Authored Acceptable Sets}{Wenhui Chen, Jianlin Chen, Ziyao Lin, Peiji Long, Chi Man Vong}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01001}{From AI Technical Debt to Agentic Technical Debt: A Systematic Mapping of Root Causes and Manifestations in Agentic AI Systems}{Muhammad Tukur, Hayatullahi B. Adeyemo, Tao Chen, Nour Ali, Anis Zarrad, Marco Agus, Rick Kazman, Rami Bahsoon}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CR, cs.SE.

本文针对现有AI Technical Debt (AITD) 模型仅适用于静态组件级架构、无法刻画Agentic AI系统动态涌现行为的问题, 提出Agentic Technical Debt (AgTD) 概念, 用以描述因自主推理、多智能体协作与工具编排而产生的技术债积累与放大效应. 作者基于先前对31种AITD根因的系统综述, 采用理论驱动的转换方法(直接转换、情境转换与表现扩展) , 首次系统建立了AITD到Agentic表现的映射, 揭示其如何演化为记忆不一致、编排脆弱性、级联失效及不安全自主决策等系统级负债. 研究表明技术债已超越软件制品, 延伸至智能体行为、协调机制及智能体-工具-环境交互, 并对AI TRiSM中的可信性、治理、安全与运营韧性产生显著影响. 该工作为自主多智能体AI系统中的技术债管理提供了转换框架、分类体系与研究议程, 奠定了AgTD作为软件工程基础构件的地位.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01008}{Toward Fine-Grained Forgetting:Attribute Unlearning for Multimodal Large Language Models}{Junkai Lin, Junkai Chen, Siqi Hou, Yuhao He, Ruiqi Liu, Chenhan Jin, Shengze Xu, Tieyong Zeng}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01049}{FactorJEPA: Factorizing Monolithic Futures into Layout-Agent-Interaction Channels for Crowded and Chaotic Global South Urban Worlds}{Kapil Wanaskar, Gaytri Jena, Aman Chadha, Vinija Jain, Vasu Sharma, Amitava Das}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CV, cs.LG.

针对Global South城市中高密度、混乱的DENSEWORLD场景, 已有JEPA类world model难以刻画异构agent间的密集交互与部分可观测性, 导致未来状态预测的可靠性不足. FactorJEPA将monolithic future分解为layout、entity与interaction三个独立channel, 并通过visibility gate保留被遮挡的agent信息, 同时抑制跨因子捷径. 该方法在Future-frame L1、Causal L1和Mask-ratio slope三项指标上均优于baseline, 且在2B与1B的V-JEPA 2.1骨干上排名一致(rho=0.895-0.978) . 此外, 模型暴露了可复现的Motion cosine权衡, 并公开了DENSEWORLD-115k数据集与surgery-trained checkpoints.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01050}{Don't Offer What Can't Be Done: Deterministic Executability Gating for LLM Skill Selection at Scale}{Ortal Ashkenazi, Vitalii Kloz, Mykhailo Ulianchenko}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL, cs.SE.

该论文解决生产环境中LLM agent从大规模技能库做选择时的关键问题: 仅凭语义相关性无法保证技能在当前account state下可执行, 导致模型可能选中无法完成的技能. 作者为Wix的Helpmate设计了三阶段选择流水线: 先由recall-oriented语义匹配器筛出相关消息, 再由deterministic executability gate基于与技能相同的exit predicates剔除满足硬停止条件的候选


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01056}{Control Under Compression: Reliability Frontiers for Tool-Using Agents}{Yinghan Hou, Zongyou Yang}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL.

该论文针对工具使用型language-model agents的Agent Control Context(ACC) 压缩问题, 指出传统压缩评估只关注token缩减而忽略了压缩后控制指令在可执行层面的可靠性, 因此将ACC压缩重新定义为runtime-reliability问题. 作者提出环境验证基准CompressAgent, 在九个ACC、三个任务族、三个Qwen API模型等条件下进行了15,525次运行, 发现压缩成功率随保留上下文比例呈非线性且依赖方法的变化: 75%保留时generic rewriting与section-based compression成功率接近92%–93%的完整上下文基线, 但35%时分别降至47.0%与19.9%, 而10%–25%区间内可执行协议高度脆弱. 进一步失败分析表明压缩主要引发tool-execution与action-parsing错误, 且不同ACC间可靠性差异显著, 因此不存在通用最优压缩器, 必须针对具体上下文进行单独qualification.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01075}{Role-Decoupled Attention Residuals: Separating Matching and Content Retrieval Across Depth}{Kehan Wang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01092}{Inter-Residue Geometry Attention for Antibody-Specific Epitope Prediction}{Chuanliu Fan, Nan Yu, Junjie Wu, Guohong Fu}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01112}{Fighting Fire with Fire: On the Feasibility of Protecting Exercises Against AI Cheating}{Tobias Braun, Jonas Grebe, Louis Rethfeld, Marcus Rohrbach}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01128}{MA-HEAD-Net: Adaptive Rule-Guided Multi-Agent DRL for AoI Minimization in UAV-Assisted Emergency Networks}{Yixin Zhang, Zhuohui Yao, Wenchi Cheng, Walid Saad}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.

针对灾后UAV辅助应急网络中的信息新鲜度问题, 该文以age of information (AoI)最小化为目标, 在Markov-modulated Poisson process描述的突发流量与finite blocklength传输模型下, 将UAV轨迹控制、用户调度及checkpoint-interval选择建模为multi-agent decision problem. 作者提出MA-HEAD-Net, 一种adaptive rule-guided multi-agent deep reinforcement learning框架, 其核心是在gated multi-head policy中融入通信域规则先验, 并用adaptive gates动态调节规则与学习策略对子任务的贡献, 最终以multi-agent proximal policy optimization联合优化. 仿真表明, 相比代表性multi-agent DRL基线, 该方法提升了策略收敛效率, 并在动态场景中获得更低的AoI.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01149}{PATH-Bench: Path-Dependent Evaluation of Lifelong Agents}{Xidong Yang, Xingyi Zhang, Wenhao Li, Wenyan Liu, Junjie Sheng, Yun Hua, Wei Yin, Tao Fang, Chuyun Shen, Xiangfeng Wang}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本文针对终生 LLM agents 在外部学习状态(如 memory 与 skills) 下缺乏路径依赖性评估的问题, 构建 PATH-Bench 基准, 通过 multi-model in-context learning 估计有向任务关系, 并设计包含 helpful/interfering histories 的 probe-centered 序列以度量 forward transfer、backward transfer 与 forgetting. 实验表明经验效用同时取决于经验表示方式与任务交互结构, 且强迁移不保证强保持, 后续经验可能重塑早期获取的知识. 基于此提出 Selective Experience Use (SEU) harness, 通过过滤干扰项并选择性利用累积经验, 在多数设置下降低 forgetting 并提升 forward transfer, 为设计更稳健的 lifelong agents 提供了可控评估框架与实用指导.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01175}{The Graph Language: How Knowledge Graphs Speak to Large Language Models}{Giuseppe Pirrò}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01183}{Co-evolution of social reward and punishment under institutional interventions}{Van An Nguyen, Vuong Khang Huynh, Hoai Thuong Nguyen, Duc Tin Duong, An Nguyen Gia, Tat Kien Nguyen, Huu Loi Bui, My Nguyen Tra, Ho Nam Duong, Ba Thanh Phan, Thanh Vo, Dinh Anh Trung Hoang, Adeela Bashir, Zhao Song, Manh Hong Duong, Le Hong Trang, Anh Han}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.GT, cs.MA, math.DS, nlin.AO.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01193}{Humans Are More Diverse: Frontier LLMs Show Extreme Policies in Idealised AI Development Races}{Phu Hoa Pham, Duy Minh Dao Sy, Trung Kiet Huynh, Phu Quy Nguyen Lam, Chi Nguyen Tran, Minh Trung Le, Phong Hao Le, Dinh Nam Nguyen, Thien Ky Nguyen Dong, Elias Fernandez Domingos, Le Hong Trang, Anh Han}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CY, cs.GT, cs.LG, cs.MA.

该论文针对AI发展竞赛中的multi-agent safety dilemma, 构建了一个2至5玩家的repeated game, 用于检验LLM agent在策略安全行为中的可靠性. 作者引入audit gate验证游戏引擎、规则记忆、state tracking与payoff计算, 发现即使规则记忆良好, agent的state tracking与expected-payoff计算仍可能薄弱, 且改变算术提示或响应表示会改变动作序列. 与evolutionary game-theory benchmark及人类数据对比后, 结果显示不同模型在动作轨迹、对对手响应及竞赛位置响应上差异显著, 且3至5人竞赛中的模式具有模型特异性. 研究强调对multi-agent AI race模拟必须进行validity check和trajectory-level analysis, 否则不能轻易将输出视为策略性、类人或安全感知的.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01197}{Reputation-driven Cooperation in Lattice-based Decentralized Federated Learning through Evolutionary Game Theory}{Phuc Hoang Truong Huynh, Dung Tran Vinh, Khoa Duc Anh Lam, An Nghiem Nguyen Truong, Uyen Nha Tran Bui, Khang Nguyen Dinh, Bao Nguyen Le Gia, Minh Le Nguyen Nhat, Manh Hong Duong, Anh Han, Thi Ai Thao Nguyen, and Le Hong Trang}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CR, cs.DC, cs.GT, math.DS.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01223}{Perspectives on Tsallis Statistics for Artificial Intelligence}{Kleyton da Costa, Bernardo Modenesi}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CE.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01233}{CT-PrepAgent: Bounded Policy and Controlled Execution for Adaptive CT Data Preparation}{Xiaolin Fan, Yue Pei, Yingying Zhang, Haogang Zhu}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对异构CT采集和多样化下游任务导致固定数据预处理流程迁移性差的问题, 现有方法依赖人工或数据集特定规则, 难以适应采集条件与分析目标的变化, 而LLM-based agent在可自适应CT数据准备中的潜力尚未被挖掘. 为此提出CT-PrepAgent, 通过deterministic inspection构建结构化data-task profile, 由bounded policy选择合法的DICOM series或预处理profile, 并采用controlled execution flow对决策进行guard、resolve、execute和verify, 在启用时以bounded recovery处理故障, 否则安全隔离. 在三个公开CT分割任务上, 该方法获得最高macro-average Dice; 在两个私有raw-DICOM队列中, verified output yield从61.7%提升至70.0%, 且公共输出上的registration metrics相近. controlled fault与replay测试验证了bounded recovery、safe quarantine及policy-free replay在测试故障和漂移设置下的有效性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01234}{Learning What to Remember and What to Internalize in LLM Self-Evolution via Adaptive Memory-Parameter Coordination}{Tianyun Ji, Zhenya Huang, Jiayu Liu, Zirui Liu, Yu Su, Hongbin Pei}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本文针对LLM agent在动态环境中自进化时, harness-based外部记忆与parameter-based参数内化两种机制各自在灵活性和性能上的权衡问题, 提出统一框架COVE. COVE通过task-aware routing将不同任务和知识类型匹配到合适的学习通道, 并引入stage-aware scheduling与knowledge optimization协调两者, 从而将自进化视为有选择的协同过程而非经验的无差别积累. 实验表明, 在多种任务类别下COVE相比单通道进化策略取得了更鲁棒且更高效的性能提升.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01319}{Cognitive Demand Steering for Adaptive Meta-Reasoning in Large Language Models}{John Scoville, Shengzhuang Chen, Yejin Bang, Stefan Winzeck, Jonathan Richard Schwarz}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01324}{G-ReAct: Graph-Guided Deep Search via Structure-State Co-Evolution}{Shaoxiong Yang, Mengyuan Zhang, Shaojun Lin, Chao Li, Wei Liu, Kun Shao, Jian Luan}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01341}{402Pilot: An x402 Decision Layer for Autonomous Agent Micropayments}{Yin Li, Yanbo He, Boo-Ho Yang, Rav Lawana, Ziyue Li, Wei Zeng, Jing Tang, Fugee Tsung}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01344}{Agentic Stage-One Stellarator Optimization: Autonomous Multi-Objective Search for Finite-Beta Equilibria}{Tingjia Zhang, Hongke Lu, Zhuoran Meng, Runlai Xu}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01361}{High-Stakes Decisions with Language Models: Insights from Emergency Triage}{Khurram Yamin, Christopher Kelly, Bryan Wilder, Eric Horvitz}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01369}{CRAFTS: Collaborative Role-Adaptive Fine-Tuning of LLM Agents for Chemical Process Simulation}{Ziyun Zhang, Yuxin Lin, Eldin Wee Chuan Lim, Xinghao Ding}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.MA.

CRAFTS针对化学过程模拟中人工构建可执行模型的高成本与易错问题, 将LLM Agent的构建流程拆解为七个有界角色, 并在各阶段间设置确定性IDAES/Pyomo工程门控, 仅对视觉、拓扑与规格三类角色做微调, 其余角色使用未调优的Qwen模型, 使每个阶段输出带类型的中间表示(如VisualGraphIR、TopologyIR、SpecIR) . 该方法利用自然语言请求、PFD证据与化工知识, 通过角色专业化与typed contract逐级完成需求理解、拓扑提取、规格赋值及调试优化, 从而提升流程建模的可靠性. 在自建的OpenIDAES-450数据集上, CRAFTS在冻结的82例测试集上完成了91.5%的验证与执行合同, unit、stream和directed-connection F1分别达到0.815、0.791和0.782, 验证了角色分工与确定性门控对自动化过程建模的有效性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01377}{CraftAlign: Feature-Grounded Evaluation and Revision Guidance for AI Stories}{Yang Yang, Boyun Xu, Shaofeng Liang, Yun Han, Zining Zhong, Songning Lai, Kaishen Yuan, Yutao Yue}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01389}{KoVRE: Training an Efficient Embedding Model for Korean Visual Document Retrieval}{Yongbin Choi, Gyuho Shim, Youngjoon Jang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01414}{No Single Neuron of Failure: Distributed Safety Alignment Against White-Box Attacks}{Simiao Xie, Chuancheng Shi, Shangze Li, Wenhua Wu, Fei Shen, Ying Zhou, Zhiyong Wang, Tat-Seng Chua}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CR.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01418}{Reusing Rollouts under Policy Lag: Prefix-Normalized Policy Optimization for LLM Reinforcement Learning}{Wenhao Zhang, Yibo Xie, Rui Wang, Jiahua Yang, Lei Jiang, Zibo Yang, Yawei Wang, Jiali Xu, jasperawang, Haoyang Long, Huan Xiong, alantzhao}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01423}{Scoring Rules! Statistical and Strategic Alignment for Text Evaluation Metrics}{Shengwei Xu, Yuxuan Lu, Yifan Wu, Jason Hartline, Grant Schoenebeck}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.GT, cs.LG.

该论文研究reference-based text evaluation metrics在作为优化目标时面临的strategic gaming问题, 指出仅关注与人类评分的statistical correlation不足以保证metric的可靠性. 作者提出两个对齐概念: statistically aligned(与人类评分相关) 与strategically aligned(抵抗不增加任务相关信息的扰动) , 并设计包含human-rating correlation、degradation sensitivity和manipulation robustness的测试原则. 他们基于mutual information构建统一设计框架, 通过四个选择(information measure、estimation method、text representation、prediction mechanism) 分解现有与新metric. 实验表明LLM-as-a-Judge虽correlation高但易被操纵, 而mutual-information-based metric显著提升robustness, 且新提出的metric在整体robustness上最优.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01430}{MRAFnd: Multimodal Retrieval-Augmented Framework for Zero-Shot Fake News Detection}{Lehan Zhang, Yinlei Cheng, Shiqi Hu Yiheng Zhou, Shangxi Li, Naidong Zhao}\textbf{cs.AI}.

A Reusable Framework for Per-Modality Failure Analysis in Multimodal Clinical AI}{Quang Bui, Shlok Jaiswal, Samuel Paik-Heintz, Kevin Zhou, Kaushik Madapati, Krittaphas Chaisutyakorn, Noah Dane Hebdon, Dimitrios Proios, Sebastián Andrés Cajas Ordóñez, Kacper Dobek, Boya Zhang, Aly Dhedhi, Ahram Han, Kushul Reddy Palakala, Rahul Gorijavolu, Jacques Kpodonu, Leo Anthony Celi}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01463}{Where Reasoning Diverges: Localized Multi-Agent Debate}{Weijun Gao, Xiang Ding, Tiancheng Xing, Haoyang Liu}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.MA.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.01521}{MineGrad: Gradient Inversion Attacks on LoRA Fine-Tuning}{Hasin Us Sami, Swapneel Sen, Basak Guler}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01543}{V-Mem: Modality-Routed Retrieval for Long-Term Multimodal Agentic Memory}{Dingyi Kang, Dongming Jiang, Yi Li, Guanpeng Li, Bingzhe Li}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL, cs.CV, cs.IR.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01548}{Emergence Invariance: From Symbolized Thought to Interface Refinement}{Yi Liu}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01558}{Securing Agentic AI: From Per-Action Checks to Trajectory Assurance}{Alireza Lotfi, Subangkar Karmaker Shanto, Imtiaz Karim, Elisa Bertino}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CR, cs.MA.

该论文聚焦LLM-based Agent的security问题, 指出安全不能仅依赖单个action的正确性, 而需保证整体行为轨迹满足系统级constraints与safety invariants. 作者提出一种统一视角, 将安全视为架构、协议和runtime的可验证属性, 而非附加的guidance层, 并覆盖从单智能体输入攻防到多智能体身份信任、以及供应链完整性与可观测性等全栈挑战. 最终目标是实现行为的behavioral containment, 即通过形式化验证与运行时监控确保任意action序列的组合不违反全局规则. 该roadmap为可信自主Agent部署提供了系统化的安全框架.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01559}{Does the Competitive Component of Adversarial Self-Play Improve Legal Reasoning? A Controlled Negative Result}{Miseog Shawn Kim}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01589}{Is More Privileged Information Better? From Solution Traces to Problem-Solving Structure in Self-Distilled Reasoning}{Xuyang Zhao, Liting Zhang, Zichen Xu, Zhihu Wang, Xu Caiyue, Shiwan Zhao, Qicheng Li}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01593}{Latent Thought Credit: Multi-Answer Credit Assignment for Latent Reasoning}{Xuyang Zhao, Liting Zhang, Zichen Xu, Yong Chen, Wenjia Zeng, Shiwan Zhao, Qicheng Li}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01604}{Post-Training on Office Work Improves Software Engineering: A Behavioral Account of Cross-Domain Transfer}{Logan Ritchie, Sushant Mehta, Liudas Panavas, Edwin Chen}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.SE.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01619}{When Memory Updates but Behavior Does Not: Repairing Implicit Stale Dependencies in Personalized Agent Responses}{Haofei Sun, Lin He}\textbf{cs.AI}.

Memory-augmented agents may know a stored user state is outdated yet still plan around the old value, a failure called the implicit policy adaptation (IPA) gap. The paper attributes this to draft-anchored verification, which checks what a response says rather than the stored state itself, so StateAuditor instead audits from stored state to draft by having an LLM propose old-to-new transitions and deterministic code verify provenance and chronology before allowing repair. On the STALE benchmark full protocol, strict single-query VTA achieves .736 versus .686 for the predecessor under the same judge, a paired +5.0 gain with 95% CI [+2.9, +7.2], and a third-model judge reproduces the gain; a matched control shows the gain comes from transition machinery, not extra context or calls.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01637}{Salami Attack: Stealthy Collusive Memory Poisoning against OpenClaw}{Zheng Lin, Yuzhe Huang, Zhenxing Niu, Xianmin Ye, Haichang Gao}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对LLM agent的长期记忆机制, 现有记忆投毒攻击仅依赖单条恶意记录, 忽视了多条看似无害的记忆可组合诱发危险行为的威胁. 本文提出MemCollusion框架, 采用salami tactics将对抗目标切分为微小且无害的记忆片段, 通过四种设计约束、五种理论策略及微调生成器构建记忆联盟, 实现跨会话的隐蔽投毒. 在复现Moltbook的MoltLab环境中对OpenClaw进行评估, 最强记忆节省设置下平均Memory Save Rate达81.3%, Attack Success Rate达75.0%, 且在良性记忆稀释与记忆级防御下仍保持有效.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01645}{GISAgentBench: A Practitioner-Sourced Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on GIS Tasks}{Abhinav Pothuri, Zhe Jiang, Zelin Xu, Di Yang}\textbf{cs.AI}.

该工作针对现有GIS agent评测基准多源于教科书或LLM生成、规模有限且缺乏ground truth的问题, 提出GISAgentBench. 基准从GIS Stack Exchange收集349个多步空间分析任务, 并在六类真实地理区域数据上实例化. 每个任务提供可执行的reference trajectory与精确的ground truth输出文件, 采用容差感知的确定性输出匹配替代代码相似度或VLM judge. 评测六个LLM模型发现最佳agent在严格容差评分下仅完成32.7%任务, 表明真实GIS工作流对现有agent仍具挑战性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01662}{LongCat Sparse Attention: Taming the Lightning via Streaming-aware Hierarchical Cross-Layer Indexing}{Wen Zan, Jiaqi Zhang, Jianchao Tan, Hong Liu, Cunguang Wang, Xiang Li, Duyue Ma, Guanyu Wu, Yifan Lu, Fengcun Li, Yerui Sun, Peng Pei, Yuchen Xie, Xunliang Cai}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL, cs.DC, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01665}{Allocation Before Ranking: Decoupled Token Compression for OmniLLMs}{Zhenghui Guo, Yilin Yang, Yuanbin Man, Miao Yin, Weidong Shi, Rabimba Karanjai, Omprakash Gnawali, Chengming Zhang}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.SD.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01667}{TCPO: Turn-Level Credit Policy Optimization}{Sicong Liao, Zhi Chen, Yaohua Tang}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对verifier-guided多轮LLM推理中评分不等同于信用分配的问题, 即每轮得分仅反映当前输出质量而无法衡量该轮对优化轨迹的贡献, 作者提出turn-level credit assignment方法TCPO. 其核心是将score-to-credit转化, 通过reference-based comparisons构造turn-level advantages, 包括retrospective credit捕捉相对最优历史状态的即时进步或回退, hindsight delayed credit识别延迟获益的非改进轮次, 以及selective fixed-history counterfactual estimation修正高意外性轮次. 在数学推理、代码生成和AppWorld agent任务上, TCPO在多种模型规模和verifier类型下优于或匹配最强基线, 尤其提升best-turn Pass@8并减少成功所需轮数, 表明score-to-credit转化是多轮策略优化的关键要素.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01679}{When Memory Becomes Authority: Benchmarking Authority Collapse at the Memory Consolidation Boundary}{Qiuyang Zhan, Rui Zhang, Sheng Guo, Lepeng Zhao, Zhuotao Liu}\textbf{cs.AI}.

该论文针对LLM agents在persistent memory consolidation过程中出现的authority collapse问题, 即记忆保留了信息内容但丢失了其来源的授权约束, 导致存储的memory被赋予超出原始来源的权威. 作者构造了AuthMem-Bench benchmark, 仅改变source authority而固定focal claim与downstream task, 系统评估write-time collapse与下游授权错误. 实验覆盖7个agent-memory consolidators与7个LLM backbones, 在49种配置中有48种发生authority collapse; 缺少authority metadata时平均unauthorized-action rate达50.3%, 而自动预测并持久化authority labels可将端到端任务中该比率从16.9%降至0.0%, 且不影响benign task success. 该工作表明memory-driven adaptation必须同时保留所学内容及其可被复用的authority.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01684}{GABench: A Comprehensive Benchmark for Evaluating LLM Agents on Graph Analysis Tasks}{Jiarui Tan, Zhongjian Zhang, YaBo Guo, Jiawei Liu, Yujie Xing, Muhan Zhang, Cheng Yang, Chuan Shi}\textbf{cs.AI}.

GABench 针对现有 LLM graph benchmark 覆盖任务与图类型有限、且多将图分析建模为基于文本的问答而无法评估端到端 agent 能力的问题, 构建了一个覆盖三类图与四类任务(graph retrieval, graph theory, graph machine learning, graph open-ended QA) 的综合基准, 并提供 84 个可执行工具以支持 agent 对图数据进行访问与操作. 借助自动生成流程, 该基准包含 10,400 个可验证 ground truth 的任务, 用于测试不同 LLM 与 harness 的 agentic 表现. 实验表明现有 agent 在复杂图分析上仍力不从心, harness 的选择显著影响性能但现有 harness 均有局限, 并且任务成功更依赖 tool-call 的质量而非数量, 这为提升 LLM agent 在图分析场景中的 reliability 提供了可操作的指导.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01710}{Beyond Single-Use Tokens: Durable Authorization State for Replay-Resistant LLM Agent Actions}{Jinghan Xu, Longze Fan, Zeyuan Wang, Xinjin Li, Hankai Liu}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本文针对LLM agent在replanning、retry、delegation及crash recovery场景中, 单一用户授权被重复执行的问题, 指出identifier-local token无法阻止fresh semantic reissuance, 因为每次重试都会获得新token identifier. 作者提出CapLease, 一个基于proposal-和authority-level防御的authorization-consumption层, 通过将用户确认绑定到canonical action, 并执行transactional Issue-Prepare-Commit状态转换, 维护monotonic durable state. 实验表明, 在并发、confirmation-replay等场景下, CapLease与Server Ledger均能阻止duplicate admission, 配合idempotent sink还可避免duplicate external effects, 从而证明durable authorization state是replay-resistant agent execution的系统级关键需求.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01711}{Constructing Executable Analytical Knowledge Representations for Meta-Analysis Synthesis Using an Agentic Harness}{Lingbo Li, Anuradha Mathrani, Teo Susnjak}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对传统meta-analysis中分析决策隐含在代码或模型输出中、难以独立验证的问题, 本文提出Executable Analytical Knowledge Representation (EAKR), 将证据分配、对比设定、效应量选择等知识显式建模为机器可执行结构. 作者设计了agentic harness MetaSynDec, 由large language models提出结构化更新, 并由确定性服务执行schema验证与统计计算. 在58个合成单元上, 57个成功执行统计, 38个达到完整reference object fidelity, 且与直接LLM生成相比, 在reference synthesis-structure agreement上显著更优(57/58 vs 23/58, p<0.001) . 结果表明EAKR结合agentic workflow能提升meta-analysis合成的可靠性、可追溯性与方法论一致性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01718}{LaCache: Robust Semantic Caching for LLM Serving}{Jiacheng Liang, Yuhui Wang, Tanqiu Jiang, Ting Wang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01735}{DAPD: Dual-Anchored Policy Distillation}{Jianyu Wu, Yizhou Wang, Encheng Su, Chen Tang, Shixiang Tang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01739}{CoEvo-Mem: Co-Evolving Retrieval Policy and Memory Bank for LLM Agents}{Bowen Ye, Yongchao Xu, Zhijian Li, Xiang Yin, Junkai Ma, Wenzhao Li}\textbf{cs.AI}.

长期 LLM agent 的记忆系统面临双重挑战: 检索策略与记忆库的更新相互影响, 但现有方法常将两者割裂优化, 忽略了 retrieval 决定哪些 memory 获得反馈、而更新后的 memory 又会重塑未来 retrieval 这一闭环. CoEvo-Mem 提出将两者协同演化, 对每个 query 由 frozen LLM 生成 route-specific rewrites 与 routing prior, 再经 lightweight residual router 在线修正; 检索结果作为耦合接口, 通过任务结果和 trajectory-conditioned feedback 同时更新 memory value 与 graph relation, 并以交替固定一方更新另一方的方式来缓解非平稳性. 在七个 benchmark 上该方法达到 state-of-the-art, 验证了 retrieval-memory co-evolution 对提升 agent reliability 的关键作用.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01742}{MemSIF: From Structured Interactions to Dual-Track Fact Memory for LLM Agents}{YuFei Luo, Xiucheng Xu, Zhen Yang}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL.

LLM agent在长期交互中的记忆系统面临两类misalignment: Temporal-Structural Misalignment(TSM)与Delayed Utility Manifestation(DUM), 即时间邻近性与事件主题关联不一致, 以及写入时的重要性无法预测未来查询效用. 为此提出MemSIF框架, 包含Structured Interaction Memory, 将原始交互组织为Topical Segments与Event Trajectories以保持局部主题连贯与跨时间连续性; 同时引入Dual-Track Fact Memory, 通过CoreFact在写入时整合schema化稳定信息, ActiveFact按需形成事实并依据历史支持与查询需求促进复用. 在LoCoMo与LongMemEval-S上基于五种backbone LLM的实验显示, MemSIF在所有设置中Total ACC最高, 较最强baseline分别提升2.29%-8.79%与2.87%-6.15%, 验证了该结构化交互与双轨事实记忆机制能有效缓解TSM与DUM.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01744}{RL-Lock: Reinforcement Learning for Generating Interlocking Assemblies}{Xuyang Ma, Chaewoon Kim, Haonan Zhang, Rulin Chen, Ziqi Wang, Peng Song}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.GR.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01755}{Deferred Exposure of Future Trajectories for Verifiable Reasoning in Autonomous Driving VLMs}{Zixuan Huang, Yang Zhou, Kaixuan Wang, Guli Zhang, Hongyan Xie, Yakun Zhu, Hao Geng, Yikun Ban, Deqing Wang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01767}{Leveraging AI for fine-grained food safety risk forecasting in sparse data conditions}{Dongqi Wang, Weiwei Chen, Han Zhou, Weihua Zhou}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01772}{FRAMES: Guarded and Dual-Objective Skill Evolution for Agents in Policy-Governed Enterprise Workflows}{Xuhui Wang, Ruoqi Shu, Chen Dan, Tianhua Xu, Mengxi Luo, Yanming Mai, Bo Wan}\textbf{cs.AI}.

FRAMES 面向 policy-bound 企业工作流中的 LLM agent, 解决稀疏无标注反馈、规则修改引发回归以及推理成本与可审计性约束下的技能进化难题. 该方法从既有资产冷启动技能, 通过 consensus-based mutation 生成候选技能, 并以 accuracy-cost Pareto 选择与 anti-regression guarantee 实现闭环迭代, 同时保持 auditability. 在内部生产系统和 tau-bench 上, FRAMES 取得最优的 accuracy-cost trade-off, 且不增加推理成本或丢失可审计性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01784}{REFLEX: Rethinking MoE Inference as Refinement-Aware Compute Allocation in Diffusion Language Models}{Xiang Xia, Cheng Yan, Yiming Zhang, Jiazheng Liu, Hongyu Zhang, Wuyang Zhang}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01792}{Can You Trust the Confidence? ConfBench for Vision-Language Models on Document Extraction}{Priyashree Roy, Sujitha Martin, Mohammad Rostami, Spencer Romo, Renhao Xue, Bob Strahan, Diego A. Socolinsky, Boyi Xie, Md Mofijul Islam}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01802}{CoNav-UAV: Cooperative Dual-Altitude Aerial Navigation via Stackelberg Learning}{Junru Song, Wenhao Zhang, Yang Yang, Xuekai Qiu, Feifei Wang, Weien Zhou, Tingsong Jiang, Ying Wen, Yang Li, Wen Yao}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.RO.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01805}{CockpitHAT: Dependency-Graph-Driven Hierarchical Attribution for Embodied Multi-Agent Cockpits}{Wei Wang, Shuanghe Liu, Zhu Zhuo, Jiaqi Zhong, Xiaozhao Zhao, Xiaojie Zuo, Jie Su}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对LLM multi-agent系统在高风险embodied环境(如车载座舱) 中存在的Correctness Collapse问题, 即任务级高准确率掩盖过程级失败, 本文提出CockpitHAT, 一个层次化归因框架. 该方法用interaction DAG上的dependency-distance阈值替代positional windows, 通过embodied adapter融合多通道证据, 并在confidence-weighted analyst consensus中对高风险failures施加safety-uplift. 实验表明, 在CockpitBench上其agent-level与step-exact准确率分别达78.3%和38.2%, 在Who&When benchmark上相较text-only SOTA ECHO最高提升17.6/16.7个点, 验证了dependency-aware、multi-channel、risk-calibrated归因范式的有效性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01822}{SearchMaster: Grounded and Regulated Self-Play for Search Agents}{Wentao Tan, Qiong Cao, Jiaqi Wang, Nan Duan}\textbf{cs.AI}.

SearchMaster提出一种自博弈(self-play)训练框架, 让单个LLM在本地搜索环境中自主生成、求解并验证搜索任务, 用于训练search agent, 以避免依赖人工标注或更强的teacher模型. 针对自生成数据中的三类不可靠信号, 它设计了基于跨文档evidence chains的Evidence-Chain Generator(ECG)降低伪多跳问题, 用Search-Depth Reward(SDR)按成功rollout的搜索深度而非成功率评估难度, 并引入Over-Opening Penalty(OOP)限制过度打开文档的浅层浏览行为. 最终通过GRPO联合优化verified proposer与solver rollout, 在六个deep-search benchmarks上将Qwen3.5-9B的平均准确率从38.19%提升到51.52%, 其中BrowseComp-Plus上提升30.1个百分点, 验证了grounded and regulated self-play能生成高质量搜索agent训练数据.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01835}{Rewriting or Reweighting? A Geometric Account in Language Models}{Juntong Wang, Shengkun Yang, Xiyuan Wang, Muhan Zhang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01837}{PCSD: Persistent Consistency for Self-Distillation in Agentic Reinforcement Learning}{Chunji Lv, Yangguang Wei, Junlin Liu, Yang Gao, Ming Liu, Xinming Wang, Jinyang Wu, Guoren Wang, Changsheng Li}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对大型语言模型agent在强化学习中的稀疏奖励问题, 本文提出PCSD方法: 由于多轮交互轨迹仅获得结果级信号, 现有的on-policy self-distillation虽能提供密集token级监督, 但teacher在部分位置不可靠, 且孤立token差异或共享step权重难以刻画位置变化. P


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01847}{FOCUS: FP4 Optimization via Coupled-Relaxation and Dual-Granularity Scaling}{Xianglong Yan, Hong Liu, Chengzhu Bao, Tianao Zhang, Guanghua Yu, Jianchen Zhu, Yulun Zhang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01849}{Exploring and Bridging Knowledge Holes in Unlearned Multimodal Large Language Models}{Junxiang You, Junkai Chen, Yuhao He, Ruiqi Liu, Zhetao Guo, Shu Wu}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01850}{Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Complex Eigenfrequency Identification and Mode Structure Reconstruction of the Ground-State ITG Branch}{Dengdi Sun, Bingbing Zhang, Xiao Wang, Zikang Yan, Yuqiang Tao, Qingquan Yang, Guosheng Xu, Jin Tang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01856}{EchoChange: A Diffusion Language Model with Dual Pass Remasking for Factual Remote Sensing Disaster Change Captioning}{Dongwei Sun, Bowen Yao, Yujie Zhang, Pei Liu, Jing Yao, Xiangyong Cao}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01862}{Wnuan: Staged Post-Training for Question Answering over Proprietary Enterprise Knowledge}{Xiaofeng Shi, Xiaosong Qiu, Wenxin Ma, Qian Kou, Yiming Pan, Longbin Yu, Ying Liu, Haiping Wang, Hua Zhou}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01875}{ReasonCast: Towards Explainable Time Series Forecasting with Reasoning}{Seunghan Lee, Jun Seo, Jaehoon Lee, Junhyeok Kang, Sangjun Han, Sungdong Yoo, Minjae Kim, Tae Yoon Lim, Dongwan Kang, Hwanil Choi, Soonyoung Lee, Wonbin Ahn}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01904}{CoEvoKG: Co-Evolving Knowledge Graphs with Self-Evolving Search Agents}{Zhaoyang Li, Zenghuang Fu, Qiuyuan Ai, Ping Jiang, Haoyu Wu, Minghui Wu, Chenxu Zhao, Jie Song, Guannan He}\textbf{cs.AI}.

CoEvoKG针对search agent在self-play训练中丢弃已获取知识的问题, 将knowledge graph同时用作可验证任务来源和持久化证据记忆, 联合训练task generator与search agent. Generator从graph中采样entity chains生成multihop问题, Agent依据答案正确性与graph evidence支持度获得reward; 搜索成功后, 检索到的evidence经验证去重写回对应节点和边, 使后续迭代的任务生成与reward计算复用增强后的graph, 形成模型进化与知识积累的闭环. 在NQ、TriviaQA、PopQA、HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和Bamboogle六个QA benchmark上, 以Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct和Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct为backbone, 宏观平均准确率较基础模型分别提升+11.2、+10.1和+11.6点, 并在相同训练预算下超过competitive self-play和RL基线.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01913}{Diagnosing Search Behavior and Failure Modes in Long-Horizon Search Agents}{Qi Liu, Jiaxin Mao, Fengbin Zhu, Tat-Seng Chua}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL, cs.IR.

本文通过轨迹级诊断研究长程搜索agent的行为与失败模式, 在固定retrieval model和evaluation harness下, 基于人类标注的文档级相关性判断, 将agent行为分解为证据检索与证据利用两个阶段, 并据此区分retrieval gaps与utilization gaps. 实验表明, 搜索次数与答案质量仅弱相关, 答案准确率更依赖于检索证据的累积recall; 有用证据常早期出现, 而agent倾向于持续低效搜索. 最佳agent能减少冗余查询, 研究为改进query formulation、evidence selection和stopping criteria提供了实用方向.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01926}{ProWorld: Progress-Aware Hyperbolic World Models for Long-Horizon Visual Goal Reaching}{Zihan Liu, Yuzhe Zhuang, Yuanzu Li, Wanshuang Gou, Jiahong Liu, Min Zhou, Menglin Yang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01937}{A Contractualist Argumentation Framework for Moral Decision-Making}{Luis Marcos-Vidal, Giulio Antonio Abbo, Tony Belpaeme}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CY.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01995}{Long-Horizon Autonomous Architecture Research with a Language-Model Agent: A Behavioural Case Study}{Aon Safdar, Mohamed Saadeldin}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本研究针对单一通用大语言模型在长期自治神经架构搜索中的行为表现, 旨在检验模型能否独立完成从假设提出到实验记录的完整研究流程. 作者为agent配置了工具面(如实验跟踪、文献访问和持久记忆), 并通过人工划分三个阶段逐步扩展其操作空间. 在约100次顺序实验中, agent将非标准Vision Transformer从弱基线提升为高效模型, 并在ImageNet-1K上达到可用但未达SOTA的水平, 同时发现: 效率提升呈明显的阶段结构, 单一早期假设贡献最大, 而commit-or-discard评估规则导致贪婪增量偏好, 且agent能推翻标准设计选择. 结论表明工作流设计对自主研究的影响至少与模型能力相当.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02005}{Evolving in the Agent Jungle via History-Informed Opponent Awareness}{Zhaofeng Zhang, Linhan Xia, Rui Liu, Yihao Wang, Binrui Shen, Shengxin Zhu}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对 multi-agent 环境中对手策略持续演化导致传统 skill-revision 方法基于过时参照系的问题, 本文提出 OASE (Opponent-Aware Selective Evolution) 机制: 通过利用对手策略的历史快照作为锚定条件, 在同一环境下对候选 skill 与现有 skill 进行 paired comparison, 仅当估计收益增益超过接受阈值时才采纳候选修订. 在 first-price auctions 与 private-cost Cournot competition 两类决策场景中, 相比 Reflexion 风格基线, OASE 在显著减少 skill revision 次数的同时降低了最终 equilibrium distance, 并以 evidence-anchored selection 取代盲目更新, 实现了对手持续演化下的稳定高效适应.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02009}{HALT: Verification-Aware Stopping for Retrieval-Augmented Search Agents}{Daeyoung Roh, Donghee Han}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02011}{Before Reasoning Fails: Pre-Evidence Procedural Failures in Agentic RAG}{Daeyoung Roh, Donghee Han}\textbf{cs.AI}.

Agentic RAG系统可能在证据条件推理被测试之前就失败, 表现为agent检索到候选片段但最终未检查证据, 这种失败模式是轨迹层面的程序性缺陷. 作者利用保存的tool-call traces、retrieved evidence、read passages和final answers将错误答案分解为pre-evidence discipline failures和post-gold-read failures, 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue的12,000条配对轨迹上发现两种失败类型基本不重叠, both-trigger率仅为11.2%-13.1%. 他们提出Read-Gate, 一个最小运行时不变式, 强制agent在搜索后、最终回答前必须阅读证据, 使原本跳过阅读的轨迹的LLM-Acc提升14.9-19.9点, 完整最小推理单元上提升3.2-9.4点. 结果表明证据收集应作为轨迹级控制问题独立于答案侧推理进行评估, 且更大的隐藏思考预算并不必然增加证据检查.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02024}{EduZone: A Framework for Evaluating LLM Safety for K-12 Students and Teachers}{Junyeong Park, Jieun Han, Haneul Yoo, So-Yeon Ahn, Jinsung Yoon, Alice Oh}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CY.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02026}{HPFA: Hypergraph-Based Paired Failure Attribution for LLM Reasoning}{Runchuan Zhu, Hongbin Lai, Bowen Jiang, Junrui Zhang, Zhangheng LI, Ostap Kilbasovych, Junyuan Hong}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02063}{Cross-Fitted Residual Utility for Primary-Preserving Cognitive Decision Correction in Automatic Modulation Classification}{Linzhuo Han, Zongyong Cui, Houbiao Li}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02087}{Instruction-Conditioned Exploration with Asymmetric Reinforcement Learning and Self-Distillation}{Jim Dilkes, Vahid Yazdanpanah, Sebastian Stein}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02097}{Fetch-then-Explore: Decoupling Selection from Extraction over a Persistent Workspace for Search Agents}{Qi Liu, Yiqun Chen, Zidan Chen, Yan Gao, Yi Wu, Yao Hu, Jiaxin Mao, Fengbin Zhu, Tat-Seng Chua}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.IR.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02113}{MemArbiter: Decision-Time Memory Arbitration for Long-Horizon LLM Agents}{Jiajun Dong, Yutao Hu, Fengrui Fan, Shihan Dou, Yueming Wu, Deqing Zou}\textbf{cs.AI}.

MemArbiter 针对 LLM agent 在 long-horizon tasks 中因 memory management 不当导致 action-relevant information 无法有效指导当前决策的问题, 提出 Memory-Action Gap 并聚焦其由 memory 组织与呈现引起的部分. 该方法将交互历史分解为 atomic items, 归入五个 functional Memory Banks, 并通过 bank-level demand、item-level relevance、focal-ambient representations 以及 temporal presentation gate 动态控制 memory salience. 在 ALFWorld 上以统一的 per-step memory budget 对比 Flat Retrieval 和 Flat Recency, MemArbiter 在 500 和 750 token 预算下分别达到 82.8% 和 92.5% 的 success rate, 超出最强 baseline 20.9 和 25.4 个百分点, 同时改善了 post-failure recovery 并降低了 failed-action repetition 与 state-action recurrence. 这表明 function-aware memory arbitration 能有效弥合可访问信息与当前决策之间的鸿沟.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02143}{Beyond Solution-Centric Search: Adaptive Inquiry and Knowledge Revision for Autonomous ML Engineering}{Shaokang Fu, Yulong Tao, Linbo Jin, Jiarong Zhao, Qiming Shi, Tianjun Pan, Haonan Li, Chengyu Wang, Jia Wu, Chengfu Huo}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对长周期机器学习工程任务中LLM agent因依赖solution-centric search而信息获取与管理受限的问题, 该文提出信息范式, 将系统对任务的动态理解作为信息状态来引导方案改进. 其具体实现Iris采用inquiry-revision loop: 基于当前信息状态生成局部行动计划, 通过epistemic actions探测决策关键未知, 同时将实验结果综合为带明确scope与status的可修订claims, 并随新证据持续更新知识. 在MLE-Bench上, Iris在12小时预算下达到64.9%的any-medal率, 超越所有对比系统, 并在四项跨domain任务上展现出良好泛化能力.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02149}{Beyond the Mean: Multi-Moment Policy Optimization for LLM Reasoning}{Yijun Zhang, Yule Xie, Jiaxin Ding, Xin Ding, Fan Xu, Haoxiang Zhang, Luoyi Fu}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02154}{Auditing Data Provenance in LLM Fine-tuning via Intrinsic Distributional Fingerprints}{Zirui Huang, Yunlong Mao, Wei Tong, Tingting Wu, Xin Ge, Sheng Zhong}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02163}{From Simple QA to Deep Research: A Verifiable Benchmark Constructed through Iterative Task Evolution}{Can Wang, Haoran Chen, Haowen Gao, Hao Ding, Zhaoyang Liu, Zhiying Tu}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02171}{From Profiling to Synthesis: Benchmarking Implicit Behavioral Alignment in Personalized LLM Agents}{Jiajia Song, Bobo Li, Haiwen Yi, Zibo Ji, Meishan Zhang, Hao Fei, Min Zhang, Mong-Li Lee, Wynne Hsu}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对现有LLM Agent个性化评估依赖静态用户画像而忽略动态偏好变化的问题, 本文提出IBA-Bench基准, 基于含噪声、隐含线索和时间不一致的longitudinal interaction histories, 验证agent能否在任务执行中满足从历史中推断出的implicit constraints. 同时提出IBA-Agent框架, 通过broad retrieval与trajectory-level alignment协调冲突偏好. 实验表明SOTA LLM agent在复杂场景下仍难以实现有效个性化, 而IBA-Agent在九个应用领域显著提升了behavioral alignment.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02184}{PAC Approximation and DIRECT Optimization for Parametric Markov Models}{Zhiming Chi, Ying Liu, Andrea Turrini, Lijun Zhang, David N. Jansen}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.FL, cs.LO.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02195}{MEGRAG: Multi-Granular Evidence Graphs for Answer-Aware Multi-Hop RAG}{Weidong Bao, Yingying Sun, Jun Yang, Yilin Wang, Zili Wei, Yubin Bao, Fangling Leng, Minghe Yu, Tiancheng Zhang, Ge Yu}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02218}{PosterMELD: Multi-Agent Paper-to-Poster Generation for Controllable Design Diversity with Editable Print-Ready Outputs}{Haojie Hu, Chenhao Dang, Yaojia Liu, Hengrui Kang, Conghui He, Weijia Li}\textbf{cs.AI}.

PosterMELD 针对科学论文到海报生成中现有系统不可元素编辑或成本过高的问题, 提出一种模板条件化的多 agent 流程. 其核心是容量感知的 slot 在渲染前引导写作, 并利用确定性 gate 与 VLM review 将失败路径路由至有界修复, 从而提升流程的 reliability. 该系统输出原生可编辑的 PPTX 与 PNG, 并支持显式设计控制以生成同类论文的不同变体. 在 621 篇论文上, PosterMELD 达到 81.3% 的 Print-Ready Rate, 为 P2P 的 3.4 倍、PosterGen 的 5.2 倍, 且单次请求成本仅约 USD 0.38, 保留了


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02238}{Trustworthy AI in Digital Health: A Comprehensive Review of Robustness and Explainability}{Abdullah Mamun, Shovito Barua Soumma, Hassan Ghasemzadeh}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02254}{Homebot: A Personal AI Agent for Conversational Home Assistance and Automation}{Shengyuan Ye, Yixin Zhang, Han Liang, Liekang Zeng, Jiangsu Du, Mu Yuan}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02267}{Self-Certification of Representation Adequacy: Sequential Certification at Minimum Task Loss}{Zijie Huang}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02276}{Harness-R1: Learning to Edit Executable Runtime Harnesses from Agent Failure Trajectories}{Shuai Shao, Kangning Zhang, Qingyao Li, Shijian Wang, Hao Wang, Wenxiang Jiao, Yuan Lu, Yi Guo, Weiwen Liu, Weinan Zhang}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本文针对LLM-based agent在部署中行为固定、无法从累积的失败轨迹中自动改进的问题, 提出Harness-R1, 将可执行运行时harness的编辑建模为可学习能力. 该方法用在线强化学习(group-relative policy optimization) 后训练一个专门的9B harness engineer, 以冻结的目标agent在同批次重跑中的任务成功率为奖励, 生成并验证可执行补丁, 无需更新目标agent权重. 在WebShop、ALFWorld和DBBench上, Harness-R1将vanilla Qwen3.5-9B的成功率从44.3%提升至53.6%, 对目标agent直接微调后, 专用engineer可进一步将平均成功率从59.2%提升至64.2%, 表明harness engineer与目标agent可协同进化.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02287}{SKT: Skill-Use Training at Scale via Verified Synthetic Data Generation}{Zelin Tan, Yiqun Zhang, Hao Li, Zhiyao Cui, Hejia Geng, Shao Zhang, Hangfan Zhang, Yang Chen, Xiaosong Wang, Lilong Wang, Zhenfei Yin, Shuyue Hu, Chen Zhang, Lei Bai}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02291}{Shared Prefixes, Better Credit: Adaptive Routing for Multi-Agent Reasoning}{Yiqing Liu, Zihao Wang, Hantao Yao, Wu Liu, Yongdong Zhang}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对multi-agent reasoning (MAR)中现有adaptive routing方法仅依赖query-level labels或trajectory-level returns导致的粗粒度credit assignment问题, 该论文提出TreeCredit框架: 通过从同一intermediate state扩展candidate operators构建shared-prefix collaboration trees, 并基于terminal correctness与累计额外成本为每个state-operator pair分配correctness-prioritized suffix credit, 从而将trajectory-level outcome准确归因到局部决策. 这些结构化credits被转换为state-local operator preferences, 用于训练轻量级pairwise state router, 在推理时动态选择下一个operator. 在六个reasoning benchmarks上, TreeCredit在保持精度略有提升的同时显著降低推理成本, 实现了比代表性MAR方法更优的accuracy-cost trade-off.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02295}{MechGeo: Autoformalizing and Proving Euclidean Geometry in Lean 4}{Hao Shen, Junyu Guo, Tian Cui, Yuxuan Xiao, Lihong Zhi}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LO.

MechGeo是一个基于agent的Mathlib原生框架, 旨在同时解决欧几里得几何中的忠实自动形式化与可验证证明构造问题. 其GeoFormalizer将非正式问题表示为标准化的GeoIR, 并确定性翻译为Lean 4代码, 再借助结构诊断与语义评估迭代修复候选语句; GeoProver则负责生成证明计划并选择性地将子目标代数化, 最终所有证明与反例均通过Lean内核验证. 在43道历史IMO几何题中, GeoFormalizer生成的形式化语句有29个被GeoProver成功证明, 其余14个则构造出Lean验证的反例并在专家修正后全部得到证明; 此外在LEAP的Lean-IMO-Bench上, MechGeo首次证明了12条语句并形式化反驳了另外2条. 实验表明, 该框架显著提升了较弱LLM的自动形式化能力, 并以反例引导诊断、几何推理与可信符号计算奠定了可信形式几何的实用基础.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02302}{Trajectories That Segment Themselves: Agent-Declared Boundaries as a Training Unit}{Jingxi Wei}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG, cs.SE.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02343}{Hard Constraints, Smooth Gradients: Learning Feasible Inventory Policies via Differentiable Projection}{Patrick Helm, Jan-Niklas Doerr, Joren Gijsbrechts, Stefan Minner}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02347}{Mamba with Hierarchical Memory: Solving Representation Bottleneck in Long Sequence Modeling}{Qinwen Wang, Jieping Luo, Aoxiang Qin, Ruoyu Zhao, Jianxiong Tang, Wei Zhang, Zhichao Lu, Luziwei Leng}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02351}{KC-Agent: A Dual-Process Cognitive Architecture for Efficient ML Model Improvement}{Gusseppe Bravo-Rocca, Jordi Guitart, Ajay Dholakia, David Ellison, Puneet Jain}\textbf{cs.AI}.

KC-Agent 针对生产环境中 ML 模型因 data drift 导致的性能下降问题, 提出一种受认知双过程启发的自动化模型改进架构: System 1 负责快速 pattern recognition, System 2 执行谨慎的增量更新, 并通过 structured memory 复用已发现的高效解决方案, 避免重复计算. 该架构引入 atomic change 与 rollback 机制, 确保更新过程可靠且可验证, 契合 reliability 需求. 在 NASA turbofan 及多个合成 drift 数据集上, KC-Agent 达到 76.8% accuracy 与 13.2s 最优执行时间, 分别超越 CodeAct、Tree of Thoughts、ReAct、Reflexion 等 baseline, 且 knowledge consolidation 机制较慢变体实现 91% 加速并保持更高准确率. 其策略质量经多个 state-of-the-art LLM 共识评估获 8.33/10, 验证了认知启发式自动化 ML 改进在复杂真实 drift


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02356}{SkillTrace: Traversing a Query-Skill Graph for Composable LLM Agents}{Yue Yao, Shengyuan Wang, Xin Chen, Minke Zhang, Jia He, Bingjun Luo, Tom Gedeon}\textbf{cs.AI}.

本文针对LLM agent在技能库中组合可复用技能时, 仅检索单个相关技能不足以完成任务的问题, 提出将技能检索建模为三层图结构, 包括查询间组合关系、查询与候选技能相似度、以及候选技能间依赖关系. SkillTrace通过构建语义层次、匹配查询与候选、并在技能依赖图上传播信息, 实现完整且可执行的技能组合. 在SkillsBench和ALFWorld上, SkillTrace达到53.17%和91.43%的成功率, 并在不同backbone LLM上保持一致提升, 验证了基于图的技能检索在reliability和泛化性上的优势.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02365}{Faster-WAM: Do World Action Models Need Deep Action Modules?}{Liheng Ma, Rui Heng Yang, Zhanguang Zhang, Mateo Clemente, Ziwen Hu, Tongtong Cao, Yingxue Zhang}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG, cs.RO.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02379}{Chess on Ice: Curling Tactical Decision-Making via Backward Induction and Deep Reinforcement Learning}{Patrick Oberlin, Matteo Cederle, Aren Karapetyan, Saverio Bolognani, Gian Antonio Susto, Florian Dörfler}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02391}{Cooperative Coevolution for Resource-Constrained Agentic LLM Post-Training}{Zhiyuan Wang, Shengcai Liu, Jiahao Wu, Ning Lu, Hui Ouyang, Shaofeng Zhang, Haoze Lv, Ke Tang}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.

Tool-using LLM agents生成的多轮长轨迹使基于梯度的后训练显存开销过高, 而标准evolution strategies (ES)虽免反向传播、显存友好, 但在仅少量GPU的资源受限场景下训练时间过长. 为此提出Cooperative Parameter-subspace Evolution Strategy (CoPES), 将全参数空间分解为低维子空间并协同搜索, 以提升优化效率并降低GPU-hour消耗. 在数学任务上对Qwen3.5-4B agent进行后训练, 在full-parameter GRPO最佳验证checkpoint的同等GPU-hour预算下, CoPES恢复了GRPO验证精度增益的92%, 而标准ES仅恢复67%; 其理论显存需求不足full-parameter GRPO的1/8, 且在五个benchmark的pass@k指标上一致优于标准ES和LoRA-based GRPO. 该方法在资源约束下为agentic LLM post-training提供了更优的显存与训练时间权衡.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02409}{MonitrLLM: A Community-Centered Evaluation Infrastructure for Large Language Models}{Victor Ojewale, Ro Encarnación, Suresh Venkatasubramanian, Danaé Metaxa}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CY.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02438}{xPress: Parallel Refinement for Diffusion Drafters in Speculative Decoding}{Zheng Wang, Davis Wertheimer, Yu Chin Fabian Lim, Mudhakar Srivatsa, Raghu K. Ganti, Minjia Zhang, Naigang Wang}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02441}{Agentic Commerce World: An Auditable and Verifiable Environment for Vibe Commerce}{Shicheng Fan, Mingdai Yang, Duohao Wang, Canyu Chen, Yongfeng Zhang, Hua Wei, Manling Li, Julian McAuley, Kun Zhang, Philip S. Yu, Kejing Yu, Zhiwei Liu}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02442}{Right Answer, Wrong Method: Shortcut Hacking Misleads the Evaluation of LLM Reasoning on Frontier Science Benchmarks}{Xuan Ren, Weiqi Zhai, Tianle Pu, Yihua Zhu, Yihua Zhu, Hu Wei, Bing Zhao}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CL.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02444}{ParEvalLayer: When Partial LLM-Agent Evaluations Support a Decision}{Wei-Jung Huang, Bonan Shen}\textbf{cs.AI}.

LLM-agent evaluation 常因部分任务结果过早产生 partial score, 但该分数无法保证与完整 benchmark 结论一致, 因为任务顺序、成本筛选或易例优先都可能扭曲观测样本. ParEvalLayer 作为一个 decision layer, 在给定预先比较策略下, 仅依据已观测的 paired outcomes 输出 better、not better、need more evidence 或 abstain, 并可用历史完整数据回放检验其判断的可靠性. 基于公开 benchmark 的 replay 实验表明, 在三种主流比较规则下, 部分 benchmark 仅需 15% 至 25% 的 task outcomes 即可达到与完整评估相同的决策, 而其他 benchmark 需要更多样本; 这证明仅报告 partial score 不够, 还需说明决策规则及尚未决断的比较数量.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02454}{Infinite Trace Objectives with Finite Trace Techniques: Translating LTL to LTLf+}{Christoph Weinhuber, Maximilian Prokop, Giuseppe De Giacomo, Moshe Y. Vardi}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.FL, cs.LO.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02464}{Real-Time Detection and Repair of LLM Agent Failures}{Sunny Dubey}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG, cs.SE.

该论文针对 LLM agent 在 episode 中频繁出现的循环、工具错误、目标偏移与结果伪造等失败, 提出仅基于 step telemetry 的实时检测与修复框架, 以避免使用昂贵 judge LLM 逐轮评判. 检测层采用 one-class echo-state network ensemble 结合 CUSUM alarm, 在 2,823 个真实 agent episodes 上以 5% false-alarm 预算捕获 0.71 的失败(AUROC 0.872) , 且其优势随 failure horizon 增长, 并可零训练迁移至外部 corpora. 为消除 monitor 的 cold-start 与 residual false alarm 负担, 作者加入确定性 verification(重算工具结果总和并检查必需调用) , 在 0 false positives 下捕获 60%-96%


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02491}{Long-term Measurements: Towards a Longitudinal Understanding of Human-AI Interactions}{Nicole Mitchell, Dhruv Agarwal, Maty Bohacek, Remi Denton, Roma Patel}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02502}{CMuon: Accelerating and Stabilizing Diffusion Transformer Training via Chunked Momentum Orthogonalization}{Chuyan Chen, Peng Sun, Kun Yuan}\textbf{cs.AI}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02505}{Abduction Without a Body? Representational Grounding and the Abduction Loop for Scientific Hypothesis Generation}{Michael Farmer}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CV, cs.IR.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02509}{Optimizing Minimax Regret in Uncertain MDPs with Small Sets of Policies}{Sterre Lutz, Daniël Vos, Matthijs T.J. Spaan, Anna Lukina}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02518}{Magnet: Detecting Cross-Session AI Misuse Through Capability Accumulation}{Natalie Isak, Matthew Dressman}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.CY.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.02553}{A Taxonomy of Cognitive Capability Gaps in Generative and Agentic AI}{Taye Akinrele, Sindhuja Penchala, Noorbakhsh Amiri Golilarz, Sudip Mittal, Shahram Rahimi}\textbf{cs.AI}.

针对generative与agentic AI在长期可靠推理与自适应决策上的能力缺口, 该文提出一个五维taxonomy, 涵盖persistent state modeling、goal-directed autonomy、self-monitoring、environment interaction及learning and adaptation, 以系统梳理碎片化认知功能. 其分析指出现有agent系统在状态一致性、自监督与持续学习上存在结构性不足, 导致跨时间尺度下的failure与不可靠性. 为缓解这些缺口, 作者设计了一个概念性Adaptive Cognitive Intelligence Architecture (ACIA), 整合记忆、目标推理与自监控模块, 并引入cognition-centric evaluation框架. 最终效果是提供统一研究范式与路线图, 为迈向更可靠且具认知能力的AI系统(乃至AGI) 奠定基础.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.02569}{AtumAI: A Principled Framework for Agentic Generation of Datacenter Control-Plane Policies}{Qiushi Lin, Chaojie Zhang, Íñigo Goiri, Aditya Akella, Ricardo Bianchini, Jovan Stojkovic}\textbf{cs.AI}, cs.DC, cs.OS.

AtumAI提出了一个基于agentic AI的框架, 用于自动化生成数据中心控制平面策略, 解决了现有方法缺乏形式化、不可迁移且搜索不系统的问题. 其核心包含Datacenter Task Compiler将自然语言目标编译为可机器检查的形式化搜索规范, 以及Evolutionary Design Discovery Loop结合diffusion model、evolutionary algorithm和surrogate model扩展搜索空间. 在workload placement、resource scaling和power management三类控制平面任务上, AtumAI生成策略一致优于专家设计的baseline, 并将新任务上线时间从数月缩短至仅需编写描述.


\section{cs.LG}
\arxivwithtarget{2608.00019}{Uncertainty-Aware Simulation-Based Inference for Operations Research with Large Language Models}{Liang Guo, Lin Shaochong, Shen Zuo-Jun Max, Zhang Kun}\textbf{cs.LG}, cs.AI.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00106}{Learning Compositional Meta-Routing for Agentic Workflows: An Executable Benchmark}{Natan Vidra, Alina Kapanova, Arun Kanhai, Spurthi Setty}\textbf{cs.LG}.

该论文针对Agentic workflows中控制器需从原始任务文本组合异构操作(如分解、检索、执行代码、委派、验证) 的问题, 指出现有routing工作仅孤立选择模型端点、检索深度或工具, 缺乏可执行且可机器验证的基准. 作者提出一个包含216训练、72开发、108测试及108词汇偏移挑战样本的可执行benchmark, 并设计budget-aware meta-router: 利用独立正则化logistic heads从词与字符特征预测操作概率, 经温度缩放后在route-cost与action-count预算下贪心组合. 在held-out测试集上, 该策略以43%更低成本达到100%成功率, 优于静态workflow的93.5%及one-shot router的56.5%; 但在词汇偏移挑战集上成功率降至75.9%, 落后静态路由的93.5%, 表明主要局限在于lexical generalization而非route execution, 整体为有界proof of concept而非实时LLM性能证据.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00107}{MetaRoute-Bench: Evaluating Meta-Decision Policies for Agentic Workflow Routing}{Natan Vidra, Alina Kapanova, Arun Kanhai, Spurthi Setty}\textbf{cs.LG}, cs.AI.

该论文针对agentic systems中反复出现的meta-decision(直接回答、任务分解、工具调用、委派、验证、失败恢复等) 缺乏系统评估的问题, 提出MetaRoute-Bench框架, 在共享执行模型下比较不同routing policies. 基于180个合成任务和8种策略、30对随机种子生成43,200条trace, 发现task-aware compositional policy达到79.4%成功率, 优于workload-specific static policy(76.7%) 、one-shot task routing(67.4%) 和direct answering(52.9%) , 相对静态策略提升2.7个百分点(95% CI ±2.0) , 但平均成本增加4.7%、延迟增加6.4%. \arxivwithtarget{2608.00301}{Abstention as an Action Can Kill Both the Reward Gradient and the KL Anchor: Collapse Law and Repair for Error-Penalized Reinforcement Learning}{Xujun Che, Yuchen Yuan, Weida Zhao, Chenyang Yu}\textbf{cs.LG}, cs.CL.

该论文研究基于error-penalized scoring rule(答对$+1$, 答错$-\lambda$, 弃权$0$) 的RL训练中, discrete abstention action会导致reward gradient与KL anchor的restoring force被同一gate-saturation factor同时抑制, 从而使模型在覆盖率下降时平均reward仍以$1/t$上升, 表现为虚假改进. 作者进一步证明advantage estimator在稀疏回答下会将设计惩罚替换为有效惩罚1, 使Chow阈值从$\lambda/(1+\lambda)$漂移到$1/2$. 修复方案是让模型始终输出confidence report并用strictly proper score加correctness reward训练, 仅在部署时通过阈值弃权, 从而消除gate饱和导致的梯度与anchor共灭问题. 仿真和两个规模的language model实验验证了机制, 并表明report-level training能同时提升coverage、accuracy和calibration.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00316}{Agentic Bayesian Optimization through Surrogate-Augmented Autoresearch}{Paul Brunzema, Louis Tiao, Nhat Le, Kevin De Angeli, Yao Xuan, Djordje Gligorijevic}\textbf{cs.LG}, stat.ML.

Bayesian optimization (BO) 依赖通用统计先验进行采样高效优化, 但难以编码领域先验, 而现有 LLM-based BO 方法要么将 LLM 固定为单一角色, 要么赋予其过度控制而牺牲可靠性. 该论文提出 agentic Bayesian optimization, 让 LLM agent 作为 BO 循环中的核心决策者, 同时保留 Bayesian backend 作为不确定性感知的优化基底, agent 可动态调整搜索边界、切换 acquisition function、提出定向评估甚至重构问题. 作者实现了 Sara (surrogate-augmented autoresearch agent) 及可被 agent 检查与修改的模块化 BoTorch 后端 lenz. 实验表明, Sara 无需先验知识即可达到 SOTA BO 的可靠性, 优于 LLM-based 基线, 并能利用 natural-language priors 超越标准 BO; 在动态设置中, Sara 能随需求变化在线重构完整优化问题, 这是标准 BO 不具备的能力.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00832}{AdvPlan-Bench: Adversarial Evaluation of Structured Plan-Generation Agents}{Alina Kapanova, Arun Kanhai, Natan Vidra, Spurthi Setty}\textbf{cs.LG}.

针对结构化plan-generation agents的评估常忽视对抗性环境, 即候选方案在面临另一agent搜索响应时的表现, 因此本文提出离线基准AdvPlan-Bench以支持对抗性评估. 其核心是通用评估对象: typed plan、adversarial response set、selector diagnostics以及traceable candidate-frontier metrics, 并用BLUE-vs-RED advantage与Nash-gap诊断对比对立方案. 在150个合成场景中, 采样八条响应的best-response策略将BLUE advantage从0.518降至0.486, win rate从0.900降至0.820; 离线LLM合同基线达到0.496/0.700, 而两阶段多agent council达到0.509/0.813, 三评分者rubric研究在600条记录上取得0.978的inter-rater agreement. 该基准不声称真实决策质量, 仅作为可复现的对抗性plan evaluation与critique-revision研究工具.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00881}{AOSpec: Action and Observation Co-Speculation for Low-Latency Agent Serving}{Hao Mark Chen, Jinnan Guo, Wayne Luk, Hongxiang Fan}\textbf{cs.LG}.

LLM agent在调用有状态工具时, 生成与执行仍逐轮串行, 解码加速后工具执行成为主要延迟瓶颈, 且仅对action或仅对observation进行投机预测都难以隐藏长尾延迟. AOSpec提出无损失协同投机框架, 通过Expected Value Decoding (EVD)优先预测期望延迟收益最大的observation, 并对只能由执行揭示的结果在隔离fork中预启动关键action, 再用Joint Action-State Verification (JASV)验证action及其来源状态与已提交执行一致, 从而将长程依赖从整链预测改写为目标状态验证, 打破lookahead与准确率的权衡. 在Terminal-Bench多样配置下, AOSpec相对所有实用基线将平均端到端延迟降低11.8-32.5%, p99延迟最高降低42.8%, 且收益随解码加速而增大, observation模型可零训练迁移至SWE-bench Verified.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.01004}{Who Belongs in the Eval Set? A Capability-Taxonomy-Driven Pipeline for Curating Regression Eval Sets in Agent-Extensibility Platforms}{Tezan Sahu, Aritra Das, Pankaj Mittal, Sudipta Das}\textbf{cs.LG}, cs.AI, cs.SE.

针对agent-extensibility平台, 团队面临回归评测集的预算约束: 客户持续提交领域eval集, 但平台回归集受查询数量上限限制, 现有工作缺乏平台侧流式数据筛选方案. 本文提出一种capability-taxonomy驱动的pipeline, 将每条query投影到平台所有的能力分类体系, 并基于“最小查询集覆盖最大能力signature分布”的原则, 输出admit、drop、swap或人工审核决策. 具体由三类组件实现: 混合LLM语义推断与规范提取的分类器生成(per-query, per-capability)判定; Invocation Quality rater评估查询对能力的覆盖深度, 使相同signature的新查询可替换旧条目; 基于规则决策级联的consolidator比较新增与存量回归集, 并由保守curator仅建议驱逐. 该机制与具体taxonomy无关, 可推广到任何带类型化能力分类的回归评测集构建问题, 并在Microsoft 365 Copilot的声明式agent场景中实际部署应用.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.01285}{Stop When Memory Suffices: Evidence-Conditioned Progressive Execution for LLM Agents}{Yidan Lin, Kaixiang Wang, Jiong Lou, Jie Li}\textbf{cs.LG}, cs.AI.

LLM智能体的长期记忆系统面临质量与在线延迟的权衡: 现有压缩式记忆会丢失时序与因果依赖, 而深度检索虽提升证据覆盖却增加推理开销. Router-Mem提出证据条件化渐进执行框架, 先以共享低代价retrieval prefix获取evidence, 再由轻量sufficiency router在inference时以single-token决策判断是否提前终止; 证据不足时复用retrieval hits扩展memory blocks并执行更深层聚合. 通过evidence-level supervision与rationale-conditioned representation distillation训练, 在AMA-Bench和BEAM上分别取得55.17%和38.77% score, 同时比full memory execution平均降低27.3%和25.5%的inference time.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.01918}{HarnessCompass: Guiding Automatic Harness Evolution toward Generalizable and Effective Agent Harnesses}{Luan Zhang, Ruochen Zhou, Dandan Song, Zhengyu Chen, Yuhang Tian, Jun Yang, Huipeng Ma, Chenhao Li, Guangyuan Feng, Xudong Li, Yizhou Jin, Yan Xu}\textbf{cs.LG}, cs.CL.

HarnessCompass 针对自动 harness evolution 中过度拟合演化任务、仅依赖轨迹信号以及联合优化导致组件干扰的问题, 提出了一种新型框架. 其核心方法包括: 对演化施加全局约束以强制修改具有 task-agnostic 泛化性, 引入 agent 对 harness 使用情况的第一人称主动反馈来补充轨迹信号, 并将不同 harness 组件的优化解耦后再合并, 从而减少干扰并保持协同. 在 SWE-bench Verified 上, 该框架以 GPT-5.4 为 backbone 将 Pass@1 从 54\% 提升至 66\%, 仅需 5 次演化迭代, 同时在 held-out 任务和其他模型上展现出更强的泛化能力.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.02412}{Why Large Language Models Fail at Tabular Prediction}{Marta Garnelo, Wojciech M. Czarnecki}\textbf{cs.LG}.

该论文研究LLM在tabular prediction上表现差的问题, 通过纯inference setting系统检验五种假设, 指出维度是关键因素. 作者用31个benchmark dataset的随机线性投影做对照实验, 发现LLM是唯一随维度增加准确率下降的方法, 而经典baseline保持平稳或提升. 进一步与252个经典模型比较, 显示低维时LLM行为接近local distance-based方法, 高维时无法被任何噪声增强的经典模型复现. 结论是LLM在表格数据上的能力维度敏感, 其失败机制仍是open question.

RoMeRL 针对自进化 LLM agent 的记忆系统提出两个紧密耦合的问题: 轨迹索引的 utility 空间随交互历史膨胀, 导致有限反馈被分散; 同时, 轨迹级奖励联合分配给共检索记忆, 使无关经验收到误导性更新并陷入 memory

\section{cs.CR}
\arxivwithtarget{2608.00104}{Skillsets on the Chain: A Blockchain-based Zero-Trust Framework for Agentic AI Networking}{Yayu Gao, Yong Xiao, Hao Hu, Xubo Li, Zhiwei Liu, Yingyu Li, Guangming Shi, Ping Zhang}\textbf{cs.CR}, cs.AI, cs.NI.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00118}{Deep Learning for Cyber Threat Detection and Mitigation in Healthcare-IoT}{Mirza Akhi}\textbf{cs.CR}, cs.LG, eess.SP.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00132}{Enforcing Access Control in Distributed Version Control Systems}{Xin Xu, Quanwei Cai, Jingqiang Lin, Shiran Pan, Liangqin Ren}\textbf{cs.CR}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00134}{Stateful Cooperative Agents Safeguarding LLMs Against Evolving Multi-Turn Attacks}{Siyuan Li, Zehao Liu, Haoyu Li, Xi Lin, Ning Liu, Jun Wu, Jianhua Li, Mohsen Guizani}\textbf{cs.CR}.

该论文针对LLM在多轮交互中面临对手动态调整策略的对抗攻击问题, 指出现有防御本质上是被动的、难以应对演化威胁. 作者提出一个由多个专职agent协作的主动防御框架, 通过控制响应节奏提升攻击成本、输出策略性模糊内容误导对手, 以及对交互日志进行取证分析以识别攻击模式并动态调整防御策略. 该框架由自适应机制协调各agent应对升级威胁, 并在包含5,200个样本、覆盖八种攻击类型的EMRA数据集上验证, 平均将ASR相对降低69%, 同时维持欺骗性交互的DR超过最强基线六倍, 并使攻击者token消耗平均增加198.83%.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00202}{From Monoliths to Swarms: A Study of Attack Surface Evolution in the Transition to Multi-Agent Web Systems}{Yashaswi Malla, Sandra Siby}\textbf{cs.CR}.

大语言模型驱动的web agent正从单agent系统(SAS)向多agent系统(MAS)演进, 角色分解在提升任务性能的同时引入了SAS中不存在的新型结构攻击面, 但该攻击面尚未被系统分类和理解. 为此, 作者提出一个针对web型MAS的攻击向量分类法, 并构建测试平台WebMASLab, 在固定用户任务、工具面与浏览器环境的前提下, 仅通过外部web对手对比单/多agent架构的安全性. 他们评估了三种对抗场景在基线、prompt加固和推理增强条件下的表现, 重点提出MAS特有的Telephone Loop攻击, 利用跨agent委托形成循环任务链, 该攻击对SAS无效, 但能使Claude Sonnet 4.5、GPT-5.2和GPT-5.4在基线下平均达到80%的攻击成功率(ASR), 仅Claude Sonnet 4.6能以92%的检测率抵御. 实验还表明prompt加固等明显防御不具备通用性, 例如对某一模型可将ASR从100%降至8%,

针对LLM agent间的生成式文本隐写, 现有方法在sender-side tokens上评估恢复率, 但receiver只能看到surface text, tokenization不一致会破坏coding state并高估receiver-side恢复性能. TI-StegoAlign提出channel-guided post-training框架, 利用Bit-Consistent Supervised Objective (BCSO)放大局部coding margin, 并用Channel-Conditioned Preference Optimization (CCPO)以receiver-realistic recovery、text quality和anti-steganalysis反馈对齐完整stegotext. 该方法仅更新LoRA参数, 通信时无需tokenization-specific

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00718}{Adversarial Attacks in Multi-Agent LLM Pipelines: Unveiling Structural Vulnerabilities in Agentic AI Architectures}{Faisal Haque Bappy, Tahrim Hossain, Tarannum Shaila Zaman, Raiful Hasan, Kamrul Hasan, Tariqul Islam}\textbf{cs.CR}, cs.AI, cs.MA.

多智能体LLM流水线因缺乏跨agent边界的显式验证机制, 导致中间输出被默认为可信输入并沿流水线传播, 从而产生单agent设置中不存在的结构性安全漏洞, 如content injection、agent impersonation、plan deviation和memory poisoning. 作者基于GAIA与SWE-Bench的真实生产轨迹, 在受控多agent环境中将上述失败模式操作化, 并在GPT-5-mini、Claude Sonnet 4.5与Kimi K2.5上采用相同流水线配置进行评测. 结果表明attack success主要由流水线架构决定而非模型能力, 说明对抗脆弱性是架构属性, 应转向pipeline-level防御而非单纯依赖更强的模型.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00783}{Safety Invariants for Agents Orchestrating Irreversible State Transitions: A Four-Dimensional Formalism Evaluated on Public Ledgers}{Zhaoming Yin}\textbf{cs.CR}.

该论文针对自主agent在外部系统上执行不可逆状态转移(如跨公链转账) 时缺乏安全性保证的问题, 指出现有agent框架仅优化任务成功率而忽视副作用风险. 作者将公链上的价值移动形式化为以(wallet, chain, address, protocol)为索引的四维空间中的状态转移, 并据此证明execution fidelity定理: 在考虑规划误映射、歧义结果、重试、至少一次投递及非人类调用者等故障模型下, 会话的真实效果要么为空, 要么恰好执行一次所渲染的转移. 七条safety invariants由该保真条件推导而来而非经验枚举, 从而将用户意图是否匹配这一不可判定问题压缩为对有限对象的单一谓词, 使预览成为充分控制手段. 在N=60对抗测试中, 该技术栈相对naive-ReAct基线在两个写激进后端上提升约74个百分点, 在写保守后端上仅提升约3个百分点, 表明单一模型评估易失效; 系统已部署, 108次跨8条链的生产写操作支撑了故障分类.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.01388}{Why Formal Monitors Fail: Attack Distribution Entropy as a Coverage Bound for LTL-Based LLM Agent Safety}{Ruiyang Zhang}\textbf{cs.CR}, cs.AI, cs.LG.

基于LTL与有限状态自动机(FSA)的运行时监控器在拦截LLM agent的不安全工具调用时, 其攻击覆盖率在不同模型架构上差异极大(68-75% vs 近零), 且无法由能力分数、训练数据或提示设计解释. 本文证明, 任何固定不变式FSA监控器的recall上界由攻击分布的集中度决定, 即出现频率最高的k个触发-完成模式所占比例; 攻击分散(高Shannon熵)时, 任何可处理规模的固定不变式集合都无法达到高覆盖率. 作者提出用熵度量预测覆盖率, 并在8种前沿LLM架构上验证: GPT类与DeepSeek攻击高度集中($H \approx 0.24$ bits), 而Gemini变体攻击熵高($H \approx 2.81$ bits), 熵解释了覆盖率76%的方差(Pearson $r=-0.87$). 他们还引入了部署前的熵测试, 仅需少量攻击样本即可预测监控器覆盖率, 支持架构感知的


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01390}{Pivot: Proactive and Verifiable Threshold Oblivious Pseudorandom Functions From Isogeny Group Actions}{Abhinav Sharma, Vikas Srivastava}\textbf{cs.CR}.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.01639}{Mutate to Bypass: Autonomous Endpoint Evasion via Knowledge-Driven Multi-Agent Orchestration}{Weifeng Yuan, Wenbo Guo, Qingyun Du, Jun Chen, Feng Dong, Haoyu Wang, Yang Liu}\textbf{cs.CR}, cs.OS.

针对EDR系统的安全性评估, 现有自动化方法难以将碎片化的安全知识转化为可用的payload, 并从模糊的告警中迭代优化. 为此, 作者提出AutoBypass, 一种基于知识驱动的多智能体闭环框架, 利用Detection-Aware Knowledge Base组织威胁情报与PoC, 由agents规划攻击、生成多态代码并编译, 再通过telemetry-driven诊断引擎反馈失败证据以修正策略. 实验覆盖七个商业端点安全平台, AutoBypass全部绕过, 对Windows Defender和Trend Micro AV的逃逸率分别达90%和86.7%. 消融实验表明, 该知识库可将8B开放权重模型的成功率从27--53%提升至43--83%, 接近大型专有模型, 验证了利用公共安全知识实现EDR韧性持续自动化评估的可行性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01642}{Performance analysis of Machine learning algorithms for predicting malware}{ABM.Adnan Azmee, Pranto Protim Choudhury, Md. Aosaful Alam, Orko Dutta, Muhammad Iqbal Hossain}\textbf{cs.CR}.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.01719}{MNC: Scope-Bound Semantic Declassification for Private LLM-Agent Communication}{Jinghan Xu, Longze Fan, Zeyuan Wang, Xinjin Li, Hankai Liu}\textbf{cs.CR}, cs.AI.

Multi-agent LLM systems face the risk of leaking protected state through internal messages, tool arguments, logs, and persistent memory even when public outputs seem safe. This paper proposes Minimum-Necessary Communication (MNC), a typed semantic-declassification protocol that selects a task-sufficient disclosure from an application-defined candidate family and binds it to explicit recipient, purpose, forwarding, lifetime, logging, and memory scopes, enforced by a reference monitor across downstream operations. A history-aware extension further accounts for inference risk from repeated disclosures. Experiments show that MNC preserves authorized delivery while blocking unauthorized forwarding, logging, durable storage, and post-expiration retrieval, and that mediated disclosures propagate correctly through subsequent planning, tool use, coordination, and memory retrieval in two-backbone MAGPIE executions.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.01759}{Benign Alone, Harmful Together: Exploiting Experience Composition in Self-Evolving LLM Agents}{Bingyu Yan, Xiaoming Zhang, Chaozhuo Li, Ziyi Zhou, Yirui Qi, Litian Zhang}\textbf{cs.CR}.

Self-evolving LLM agents accumulate interaction trajectories as persistent experiences, but individually benign experiences can jointly weaken safety boundaries when composed across sessions. Existing memory attacks need direct access or explicit malicious records, so the authors propose EvoBreak, a sequential attack using benign-stage tasks: it observes the victim's distilled experiences, identifies uncovered target-relevant requirements, and adaptively collects complementary experiences before reformulating the final query to activate them jointly. To train EvoBreak, they design BreakGym, a structure-first pipeline synthesizing decomposable safety-sensitive targets, and optimize the attack via rejection-sampling supervised fine-tuning plus Hint-guided GRPO. Experiments across self-evolving frameworks, victim backbones, pre-evolution domains, and safety benchmarks show EvoBreak consistently outperforms existing attacks while maintaining high benignness, revealing benign experience composition as a persistent attack surface.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.02422}{Agentic Incident Response through Digital Twin-Enhanced Multiscale Planning}{Yiran Gao, Tao Li, Kim Hammar}\textbf{cs.CR}, cs.AI.

该论文针对当前 incident response 依赖人工 playbook 导致决策缓慢, 以及纯 LLM agent 规划因幻觉而不可靠且规划时域受限的问题, 提出了一种结合 decision-theoretic planning 与 LLM 生成的 agentic 方法. 其核心架构使用 rollout planner 在战术尺度上计算高层的安全资源分配策略, 再由轻量级 LLM agent 在操作尺度上将策略转化为可执行命令, 同时利用 digital twin 分别通过 simulation 和 emulation 支持战术规划与操作执行. 实验表明, 在三个攻击场景下, 该方法相较前沿 LLM 基线平均降低 recovery execution time 15.1%, 并提升 recovery rate 33.6%, 验证了分层规划


\section{cs.CL}
\arxivwithtarget{2607.11183}{Amplitude-Only FFN Intervention for Tool-Structured LLM Inference Method: Gated Evaluation Protocol, and Cross-Model Empirical Results}{Sheng Xu, Junhua Wang, Boyuan Huang, Ke Jia, Jiadun Zhu, Zhen Chen}\textbf{cs.CL}, cs.AI, cs.LG.

本文针对LLM作为tool-using agent时结构化输出中因参数或function-call格式错误导致的可靠性问题, 提出无需重训练的inference-time FFN干预方法. 作者先发现Orthogonal Residual Projection (ORP)虽能定位敏感的SwiGLU FFN干预位点但常有害, 转而提出Amplitude Gating (AG), 仅调制activation magnitude而不改变pretrained FFN权重方向. 通过细粒度P1/P2/P3与branch-specific P1s/P2a/P2b干预系统及分离oracle headroom与learned gates的评估协议, 在Qwen3.5-9B等模型上验证AG对tool-structured任务最有效, 如Qwen3.5-9B上tool/structured/agentic性能提升4.27个百分点, Hermes function-call约+7.6点, 但不同模型与类别需定制化routing, 且在线验证与更广跨模型评估仍待进行.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00004}{Cost-Effective Automated Judging of Natural-Language Mathematical Proofs}{Benjamin Grayzel}\textbf{cs.CL}, cs.AI, cs.LG.



\arxivwithtarget{2608.00005}{RubricReviewer: From Direct Critique to Objective and Comprehensive Rubric-Driven Peer Review}{Shuyu Guo, Wenxiang Hu, Yuyue Zhao, Yougang Lyu, Xiaohui Yan}\textbf{cs.CL}, cs.AI.

Peer review 面临巨大投稿压力, 现有基于 LLM 的评审系统存在结构缺陷: 直接将稿件映射到评审意见导致 rubric 隐式且与判断纠缠, 且无训练 agent 虽有广泛证据但缺乏判别力, 有训练模型虽继承人类判别却带有噪声和不均覆盖. 为此提出 RubricReviewer, 将 rubric 生成作为显式中间步骤, 使评审和最终评估均基于论文自适应 rubric, 并组合无训练 agent Scout 收集外部证据与人类对齐模型 Aligner 消费证据, 融合两类监督优势. 在真实投稿上的实验表明, 该方法生成的评审在全面性和判别性上显著优于现有系统, 且对对抗性 prompt injection 攻击具有最强鲁棒性, 消融实验验证了各组件的必要性.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00007}{MemoryForge: Synthesize Lifelong Memory for Human-Like LLM Agents}{Bohan Tang, Yiwen Guo}\textbf{cs.CL}, cs.AI, cs.LG.

本文针对传统基于提示的LLM agent通过静态文本描述进行人格塑造导致行为泛化、缺乏真实生活记忆的问题, 提出记忆条件化范式, 借鉴认知心理学以自传体记忆库替代抽象画像, 使冻结的LLM动态检索情境相关记忆以指导行为. 作者形式化该任务为定制化终生记忆合成, 并提出MemoryForge框架, 包含三个组件: 用于社会历史背景生成的context generator、用于保持目标身份发展一致性的life organizer, 以及平衡时间摘要与高保真情节体验的多分辨率模拟器. 在PersonaGym和SimulatorArena上的实验表明, MemoryForge生成的记忆库使冻结LLM在多项指标和多种backbone上表现出比强描述性条件化基线更接近人类的行为.


\arxivwithtarget{2608.00009}{AgentMemBench: A Systematic Benchmark for Evaluating Long-Term Memory Management Strategies in Conversational AI Agents}{Ahmed Cherif}\textbf{cs.CL}, cs.AI.

长期记忆是对话AI的关键瓶颈, 因为有限上下文窗口无法支撑跨数千轮的一致回忆, 现有方法在长时距场景下效果显著退化. AgentMemBench在统一条件下系统评估五种记忆管理策略(ICW, EKV, GEM, CBS, WAM) , 使用Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00030}{SLMs as Multi-Agent Routers: A Progressive SFT and Reinforcement Learning Approach}{Gayathri V Kondapalli, Alexander Ng, Hirsh Pithadia, Rahul Monish, Harvey Yorke, Amir Kayhani}\textbf{cs.CL}, cs.AI.

该论文解决多agent系统中查询路由的可靠性问题, 指出仅基于query意图或主题选择agent无法利用检索结果反馈, 也无法识别主题匹配但检索质量低下的agent. 作者提出用小型语言模型(small language model)先进行supervised fine-tuning, 再通过reinforcement learning联合学习agent选择与下游tool call的结构化参数生成, 并使用基于检索相关性(relevance)与query-agent主题对齐的分层reward函数. 最终该模型能根据检索表现学习agent的task-dependent suitability, 在agent-query错配子集上NDCG@10达到0.918, 显著优于仅依赖意图路由的LLM基线, 且平均NDCG@10为0.771, 选择延迟仅120.1ms.

\arxivwithtarget{2608.00218}{A Few Neurons Reveal When LLMs Misuse Tools: Sparse Detection and Selective Steering for Reliable Tool Use}{Yutong Ke, Ming Yin, Chongwen Zhao, Kaizhu Huang}\textbf{cs.CL}.

本文针对agentic LLM在工具调用中的三类failure(invalid arguments, over-calling, missing) , 发现少量failure-specific MLP neurons即可线性可分地编码这些错误状态, 从而为轻量级检测提供基础. 作者提出PRISMS框架, 利用L1正则化在贡献关键神经元上拟合sparse detector, 在pre-generation阶段或工具调用片段上实现ROC-AUC 0.86-1.00的检测性能, 且仅需1-128个神经元, 远少于dense residual-stream基线(23-627倍特征压缩) . 该共享神经元表示同时支持对工具调用行为的双向steering, 并基于预测的失败风险进行条件干预, 从而避免无条件干预的副作用. 在Qwen3, Llama, Gemma六个模型上, PRISMS将over-calling率从0.131降至0.026(降幅80%) , 并将tool-required准确率从0.689提升至0.831, 展示了跨模型家族的
